Commit Message

[FSW-6191] restructure preboot zephyr app

This patch performs restructuring of arcmgmt code as proposed in
Jira FSW-6191.
App file preboot.c moved to app/io/preboot.c which will be used common
for gaudi generations and gpu generations io die.
device specific files moved under arch folder.
ras, fit, lzo, comms and tpm are moved under lib folder

Signed-off-by: Pavan S <psreenivas@habana.ai>

+#define BTL_VER_PREFIX	"BTL version "
+#define FUSE_VER_PREFIX	"Fuse version "

+static char preboot_vers[][VERSION_MAX_LEN] = {
+	[PREBOOT_BTL_VER] = { BTL_VER_PREFIX },
+	[PREBOOT_EFUSE_VER] = { FUSE_VER_PREFIX },

+
+bool hl_shell_reset_misc(const struct device *dev, enum hl_reset_type reset, bool force)
+{
+	bool arc_pid_reset_flag = false;
+
+	switch (reset) {
+	case HL_RESET_TYPE_WD_RST:
+		watchdog_stop_feed();
+		/* no need to call reset driver */
+		return false;
+	case HL_RESET_TYPE_FW_ALL_RST:
+	case HL_RESET_TYPE_SW_ALL_RST:
+		arc_pid_reset_flag = true;
+		break;
+	case HL_RESET_TYPE_SOFT_RST:
+		return true;
+	default:
+		/* ignore request */
+		LOG_ERR("Unsupported or unknown reset request");
+		return false;
+	};
+
+	/* gracefully stops all modules' threads. Supplying value 0 to ensure
+	 * all threads will be handled. The purpose of the argument, is to indicate which
+	 * thread should not be stopped.
+	 */
+	if (!force)
+		cmd_module_shutdown(0, SHUTDOWN_MODE_SET_RESET_TIMEOUT);
+
+	/* make sure ARC0 is halted, relevant when PCIe link is down */
+	if (arc_pid_reset_flag)
+		halt_n_reset_arc(0);
+
+	return true;
+}
+
+static void preboot_version(void)
+{
+	int err;
+	int i = 0, btl_ver_sz = 0;
+
+	LOG_INF("\n%s", preboot_ver_str);
+
+
+	/* copy the preboot version */
+	for (int j = 0; j < VERSION_MAX_LEN - btl_ver_sz - 1; j++) {
+		preboot_vers[PREBOOT_BTL_VER][i++] = preboot_ver_str[j];
+		if (preboot_ver_str[j] == '\0')
+			break;
+	}
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_PREBOOT_BTL_VERSION, preboot_vers[PREBOOT_BTL_VER],
+			VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Failed to pass preboot BTL version securely ret=%d", __func__, err);
+}
+
+#define FUSE_VER_NOT_IMPLEMENT	"not implemented"
+__weak int hl_fuse_get_version(char *version, size_t size)
+{
+	int i, max_sz = sizeof(FUSE_VER_NOT_IMPLEMENT);
+	const char *not_impl = FUSE_VER_NOT_IMPLEMENT;
+
+	max_sz = size < max_sz ? size : max_sz;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < max_sz; i++)
+		version[i] = not_impl[i];
+
+	version[max_sz] = '\0';
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+struct preboot_str_size {
+	char *str;
+	size_t size;
+};
+
+void preboot_fuse_version_serialize(char *ver, size_t size,
+				    struct preboot_fuse_ver_data *ver_data,
+				    bool is_reverse)
+{
+	struct preboot_str_size ver_str[] = {
+		{ ver_data->table_ver, ver_data->table_ver_sz },
+		{ ver_data->dev_id, ver_data->dev_id_sz },
+		{ ver_data->fab, ver_data->fab_sz },
+		{ ver_data->lot, ver_data->lot_sz },
+		{ ver_data->wafer, ver_data->wafer_sz },
+		{ ver_data->xaddr, ver_data->xaddr_sz },
+		{ ver_data->yaddr, ver_data->yaddr_sz },
+	};
+	int i, j, str_index;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(ver_str); i++) {
+		for (j = 0; j < ver_str[i].size; j++) {
+			if (!size)
+				break;
+			str_index = is_reverse ? ver_str[i].size - 1 - j : j;
+			*ver++ = ver_str[i].str[str_index];
+			size--;
+		}
+		if (!size)
+			break;
+		*ver++ = '-';
+		size--;
+	}
+	/* Replace the last '-' with NULL terminator */
+	ver[-1] = '\0';
+}
+
+#define FUSE_VER_UNKNOWN	"unknown"
+static void fuse_version(void)
+{
+	size_t ver_prefix_len = sizeof(FUSE_VER_PREFIX) - 1;
+	char *version = preboot_vers[PREBOOT_EFUSE_VER] + ver_prefix_len;
+	const char *unknown = FUSE_VER_UNKNOWN;
+	int err, i;
+
+	err = hl_fuse_get_version(version, VERSION_MAX_LEN - ver_prefix_len);
+	if (err)
+		for (i = 0; i < sizeof(FUSE_VER_UNKNOWN); i++)
+			version[i] = unknown[i];
+
+	LOG_INF("%s", preboot_vers[PREBOOT_EFUSE_VER]);
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_PREBOOT_EFUSE_VERSION, preboot_vers[PREBOOT_EFUSE_VER],
+			VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Failed to pass EFUSE version securely ret=%d", __func__, err);
+}

+bool preboot_is_overlap_address_range(u64 addr, int len)
+{
+	u64 start = PREBOOT_BIN_START;
+	u64 stack_top = PREBOOT_BIN_START + PREBOOT_BIN_SIZE;
+
+	if (addr + len <= (uintptr_t)&start || addr >= (uintptr_t)&stack_top)
+		return false;
+
+	return true;
+}
+

+#ifdef CONFIG_PERSIST_NIC_EN

+int preboot_get_nic_en(u32 *nic_en)
+{
+	const struct device *eeprom_dev = EEPROM_DEV_INST;
+	struct eeprom_field field;
+	int err = 1;
+
+	if (eeprom_dev)
+		err = hl_eeprom_get_field(eeprom_dev, "NIC ena ports", &field);
+
+	if (!err)
+		memcpy(nic_en, field.buf, sizeof(u32));
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+#else
+
+int preboot_get_nic_en(u32 *nic_en)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_copy(HL_BOOT_INFO_NIC_EN, nic_en, sizeof(u32));
+
+	return err;
+}

+#endif /* CONFIG_PERSIST_NIC_EN */

+static void preboot_boot_info_set_nic_en(void)
+{
+	int err;
+	u32 nic_en_local;
+
+	err = preboot_get_nic_en(&nic_en_local);
+	if (err)
+		nic_en_local = preboot_get_nic_mask();
+
+	preboot_nic_en(nic_en_local);
+}

+u32 err_sts_reg_val;

+__weak void oob_set_boot_err(enum hl_fw_fit_boot_error err, bool is_linux_boot_fail) {}
+__weak void oob_fw_info_reload(void) {}
+
+static void preboot_process_last_boot_info(void)
+{
+	u32 boot_err = HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_NONE;
+	u32 rst_src;
+
+	if (!hl_boot_info_copy(HL_BOOT_INFO_FIT_BOOT_ERROR, &boot_err, sizeof(boot_err))) {
+		switch (boot_err) {
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_NONE:
+			break;
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_CORRUPTED:
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_ADDRESS_VIOLATION:
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_NOT_RUNNABLE:
+			cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_FIT_CORRUPTED);
+			break;
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_REVOKED_KEY:
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_SVN:
+		case HL_FW_FIT_BOOT_ERR_SIGNATURE:
+			cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_SECURITY_FAIL);
+			break;
+		default:
+			break;
+		}
+
+		oob_set_boot_err(boot_err, true);
+	}
+
+	preboot_boot_info_set_nic_en();
+
+	/*
+	 * Preserve pci disable if exist, except for PRSTN, in which it is
+	 * required to clear previous indication.
+	 */
+	rst_src = hl_rst_src_get();
+	/* device activity counter is cleared in power cycle or prstn */
+	if (rst_src == HL_PRSTN_RST || rst_src == HL_COLD_RST)
+		fw_info.device_activity_counter = 0;
+
+	oob_fw_info_reload();
+	module_fwupd_pcie_access_reload();
+}
+
+static void device_init(void)
+{
+	device_init_pci();
+	device_init_global_conf();
+}
+
+static void preboot_cpu_boot_sts_init(void)
+{
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_PREBOOT);
+	/* Enable the errors communication */
+	cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENABLED);
+	/* Enable the device status communication */
+	cpu_boot_dev_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_ENABLED);
+
+	cpu_boot_dev_sts_clr(CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DRAM_INIT_EN);
+	cpu_boot_dev_sts_clr(CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DRAM_SCR_EN);
+	cpu_boot_err_clr(CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_INIT_FAIL);
+	cpu_boot_err_clr(CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_SKIPPED);
+}
+
+void preboot_print_device_info(void)
+{
+	int err;
+	struct eeprom_field field;
+	const struct device *eeprom_dev = EEPROM_DEV_INST;
+
+	if (!eeprom_dev) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s no eeprom found", __func__);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	err = hl_eeprom_get_field(eeprom_dev, "Production Serial Number", &field);
+	if (err)
+		return;
+
+	LOG_INF("Product S/N: %.*s\n", field.size, field.buf);
+
+	if (cpu_boot_err_get(CPU_BOOT_ERR_DEVICE_UNUSABLE_FAIL))
+		LOG_INF("**** RMA STATUS SET, DEVICE IS FAULTY ****");
+}
+
+__weak int pldm_send_versions(void) { return 0; }
+
+static int send_versions(void)
+{
+	int ret = 0;
+	uint32_t module_versions_size = component_versions.modules_counter *
+		sizeof(struct hl_module_data);
+
+	strncpy(component_versions.component, PREBOOT_VER, VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	strncpy(component_versions.fw_os, PREBOOT_FW_OS_VER, VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	strncpy(component_versions.comp_name, "preboot", sizeof("preboot"));
+
+	ret = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_PREBOOT_VERSIONS,
+			&component_versions, sizeof(component_versions) +
+			module_versions_size);
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Failed to set boot info (%d)\n", __func__, ret);
+		return ret;
+	}
+
+	return pldm_send_versions();
+}
+
+__weak int dev_erom_version_get(char *version, unsigned int ver_len) { return 0; }
+
+static char erom_ver[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+
+static void erom_version(void)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = dev_erom_version_get(erom_ver, VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("failed to get erom version");
+		return;
+	}
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_EROM_VERSION, erom_ver, VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to set erom ver in bootinfo (%d)\n", err);
+}
+
+__weak void preboot_dev_spec_bootinfo_init(void) {}
+
+static void preboot_boot_info_init(void)
+{
+	preboot_dev_spec_bootinfo_init();
+	fw_info_init();
+	mem_info_init();
+}
+
+void preboot_finalize_fw_info(void)
+{
+	u64 cpu_boot_err = cpu_boot_err_get_all();
+
+	fw_info.cpu_boot_err_val[0] = (u32)cpu_boot_err;
+	fw_info.cpu_boot_err_val[1] = (u32)(cpu_boot_err >> 32);
+
+	fw_info_set();
+	mem_info_set();
+}

+__weak void hl_set_reset_cause(uint32_t reset_cause) {}
+__weak u32 hl_skip_fw_cfg(void) { return 0; }
+__weak void hl_dev_pll_init(void) {}
+__weak void hl_clock_gating_init(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_process_last_rma_info(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_device_id_init(void) {}
+__weak u32 preboot_device_id_get(void) { return 0; }
+__weak void preboot_engines_init(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_read_straps(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_read_test_mode(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_reset_init(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_cond_arc_reset(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_tpm_auth(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_validate_fw(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_rma_init(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_process_sram_bist(void) {}
+__weak int preboot_get_binning_info(void) { return 0; }
+__weak void preboot_plat_early_init(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_set_bis_compliant_mode(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_clear_pcie_mstr_axuser_cfg(void) {}
+__weak int preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(bool enable) { return 0; }

+__weak void preboot_shell_set_prompt_name(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_gpio_init(void) {}

+__weak void preboot_mctp_init(void) {}
+__weak int watchdog_init_on_start(void) {return -1; }
+__weak bool watchdog_watch_threads(void) {return false; }
+
+static void preboot_init_efuse(void)
+{
+	uint32_t data;
+	int i, err;
+
+	if (hl_rst_src_get() != HL_COLD_RST)
+		return;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < FUSE_WORDS_PER_BANK; i++) {
+		err = preboot_fuse_read(i, &data);
+		if (err)
+			LOG_ERR("Failed to read efuse word %d", i);
+	}
+}
+
+void main(void)
+{
+	int key;
+
+	device_init();
+	preboot_plat_early_init();
+	watchdog_disable();
+	if (watchdog_init_on_start() == 0)
+		main_thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+	/* Initialize internal if variables */
+	cpu_boot_if_init();
+	preboot_cpu_boot_sts_init();
+	preboot_heap_init();
+
+	preboot_boot_info_init();
+
+	hl_dev_pll_init();
+
+	preboot_cond_arc_reset();
+
+	spi_flash_init();
+	spi_flash_prot();
+	spi_flash_prot_status();
+
+	if (main_thread_desc)
+		wdt_thread_keep_alive(main_thread_desc);
+
+	/* TODO: check if locking interrtups during HW init is still nescesarry */
+	key = arch_irq_lock();
+
+	preboot_gpio_init();
+	preboot_nic_mask_serr_derr_interrupts();
+	preboot_reset_init();
+
+	preboot_device_id_init();
+	preboot_init_efuse();
+
+	preboot_clear_pcie_mstr_axuser_cfg();
+	preboot_security_init();
+	preboot_security_dynamic_init();
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_SECURITY_READY);
+
+	hl_ts_init();
+
+	if (main_thread_desc)
+		wdt_thread_keep_alive(main_thread_desc);
+
+	/* Prepare boot FW versions and eFuse data for host */
+	preboot_version();
+	fuse_version();
+	erom_version();
+	preboot_read_straps();
+
+	preboot_read_test_mode();
+
+	preboot_led_on();
+
+	preboot_get_binning_info(); /* Should be called before preboot_binning_init() in Gaudi2 */
+
+	preboot_isolation_init();
+	preboot_binning_init();
+	hl_clock_gating_init();
+	preboot_eeprom_init();
+	preboot_rma_init();
+	hl_ram_init();
+	preboot_dev_enable_caps();
+
+	preboot_set_bis_compliant_mode();
+
+	if (main_thread_desc)
+		wdt_thread_keep_alive(main_thread_desc);
+
+	preboot_process_last_boot_info();
+	preboot_process_sram_bist();
+	preboot_process_last_rma_info();
+
+	if (hl_is_watchdog_reset())
+		oob_set_wd_err(HL_FW_RESET_CAUSE_WATCHDOG);
+
+	preboot_engines_init();
+
+	if (main_thread_desc)
+		wdt_thread_keep_alive(main_thread_desc);
+
+	preboot_tpm_auth();
+
+	preboot_mctp_init();
+
+	arch_irq_unlock(key);
+
+	hl_dev_cpld_i2c_recovery_init();
+
+	preboot_print_device_info();
+	preboot_validate_fw();
+
+	send_versions();
+
+	if (main_thread_desc)
+		wdt_thread_keep_alive(main_thread_desc);
+
+	preboot_shell_activate_cmds();
+	preboot_shell_set_prompt_name();
+
+	module_handle_cmds();
+	if (main_thread_desc)
+		wdt_thread_finished(k_current_get());
+}
+
+bool preboot_error_inform(u32 major, u32 minor)
+{
+	/*
+	 * ensuring sending the first error occurrence
+	 * to reset the error just use the clr_on_read
+	 */
+	if (!pending_error.error) {
+		pending_error.major = major;
+		pending_error.minor = minor;
+		return true;
+	}
+
+	return false;
+}
+
+bool preboot_error_is_pending(void)
+{
+	return !!pending_error.error;
+}
+
+u32 preboot_error_read_and_clr(u32 *major, u32 *minor)
+{
+	*major = pending_error.major;
+	*minor = pending_error.minor;
+
+	pending_error.error = 0;
+	return *major;
+}
+
+__weak int preboot_set_sram_sf_access(bool is_enable)
+{
+	return 1;
+}

+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2024 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <zephyr.h>
+#include <common.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(app_wrq);
+
+#define PIDWRQ_STACK_SIZE	(4352 - CONFIG_HL_STACK_OVERFLOW_REDUCE_SIZE)
+#define PIDWRQ_PRIORITY		CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY
+
+struct k_work_q app_wrq;
+
+static K_KERNEL_STACK_DEFINE(wrq_stack, PIDWRQ_STACK_SIZE);
+
+k_tid_t module_appwrq_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	struct k_work_queue_config cfg = {
+		.name = "app-wrq",
+		.no_yield = false,
+	};
+
+	k_work_queue_start(&app_wrq, wrq_stack, K_KERNEL_STACK_SIZEOF(wrq_stack),
+				PIDWRQ_PRIORITY, &cfg);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(k_work_queue_thread_get(&app_wrq), thread_name);
+
+	return k_work_queue_thread_get(&app_wrq);
+}
+
+void module_appwrq_shutdown(void)
+{
+	k_work_queue_drain(&app_wrq, true);
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_appwrq_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return k_work_queue_thread_get(&app_wrq);
+}

b/lib/common/crc8.c

@@ -0,0 +1,55 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <crc8.h>
+
+/* This code was copied from from linux/lib/crc8.c */
+
+/**
+ * crc8_populate_msb - fill crc table for given polynomial in reverse bit order.
+ *
+ * @table:	table to be filled.
+ * @polynomial:	polynomial for which table is to be filled.
+ */
+void crc8_populate_msb(u8 table[CRC8_TABLE_SIZE], u8 polynomial)
+{
+	int i, j;
+	const u8 msbit = 0x80;
+	u8 t = msbit;
+
+	table[0] = 0;
+
+	for (i = 1; i < CRC8_TABLE_SIZE; i *= 2) {
+		t = (t << 1) ^ (t & msbit ? polynomial : 0);
+		for (j = 0; j < i; j++) {
+			/*
+			 * Using (i+j) % CRC8_TABLE_SIZE to ensure no out of bound write.
+			 * This is a deviation from standard library as we noticed index wrap around
+			 * not happening with arc_gnu_2019 compiler.
+			 */
+			table[(i+j) % CRC8_TABLE_SIZE] = table[j] ^ t;
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+/**
+ * crc8 - calculate a crc8 over the given input data.
+ *
+ * @table: crc table used for calculation.
+ * @pdata: pointer to data buffer.
+ * @nbytes: number of bytes in data buffer.
+ * @crc: previous returned crc8 value.
+ */
+u8 preboot_crc8(const u8 table[CRC8_TABLE_SIZE], u8 *pdata, size_t nbytes, u8 crc)
+{
+	/* loop over the buffer data */
+	while (nbytes-- > 0)
+		crc = table[(crc ^ *pdata++) & 0xff];
+
+	return crc;
+}

b/lib/common/eeprom.c

@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2022 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <device.h>
+#include <oob_if.h>
+#include <eeprom_util.h>
+#include <hl_boot_info.h>
+#include <hl_boot_if.h>
+#include <boot_if.h>
+#include <cmd_module.h>
+#include <fw_info.h>
+#include <hl_devs.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(preboot_eeprom);
+
+static struct eeprom_layout *eeprom_layout;
+
+void preboot_set_eeprom_defaults_en(u8 eeprom_defaults_on_err)
+{
+	fw_info.eeprom_def_boot_en = eeprom_defaults_on_err;
+}
+
+__weak void oob_set_eeprom_error_status(u8 val) {}
+
+void preboot_set_eeprom_err(int err)
+{
+	const char *err_msg = NULL;
+
+	fw_info.eeprom_err = err;
+	oob_set_eeprom_error_status(err);
+	cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_EEPROM_FAIL);
+
+	switch (err) {
+	case HL_EEPROM_ERR_DEV_FAIL:
+		err_msg = "EEPROM device communication failure";
+		break;
+	case HL_EEPROM_ERR_CRC_FAIL:
+		err_msg = "EEPROM data CRC failure";
+		break;
+	case HL_EEPROM_ERR_REC_DATA_FAIL:	/* is not valid for LKD */
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+
+	if (err_msg)
+		module_lkd_register_ascii_msg(LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_ERR, err_msg);
+}
+
+__weak void preboot_chk_i2c_bus_valid(void) {}
+
+int preboot_eeprom_init(void)
+{
+	const struct device *eeprom_dev = EEPROM_DEV_INST;
+	int ret;
+
+	if (!eeprom_dev) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: no eeprom device found", __func__);
+		return -ENODEV;
+	}
+
+	ret = hl_eeprom_layout_init(eeprom_dev, &eeprom_layout, false);
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_WRN("EEPROM: read failed (%d), using defaults", ret);
+		preboot_chk_i2c_bus_valid();
+		preboot_set_eeprom_err(ret);
+	}
+
+	ret = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_EEPROM, eeprom_layout->data, eeprom_layout->data_size);
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Failed to set boot info:(%d)\n", __func__, ret);
+		return ret;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}

b/lib/common/fw_info.c

@@ -0,0 +1,140 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * (C) Copyright 2023 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All rights reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <string.h>	/* for memset */
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <hl_boot_info.h>
+#include <hl_fw_if.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+#define INVALID_VERSION (~0U)
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(preboot_fw_info);
+
+struct hl_fw_info_compat fw_info;
+struct hl_mem_info_compat mem_info;
+
+typedef uint32_t *(*get_ver_cb)(void *);
+
+static uint32_t *fw_info_get_ver(void *data)
+{
+	return &((struct hl_fw_info_compat *)data)->version;
+}
+
+static uint32_t *mem_info_get_ver(void *data)
+{
+	return &((struct hl_mem_info_compat *)data)->version;
+}
+
+static void compat_struct_set(enum hl_boot_info_type type, void *data, size_t size,
+				uint32_t curr_ver, get_ver_cb get_ver)
+{
+	uint32_t *ver = get_ver(data);
+	int ret;
+
+	if (*ver == curr_ver) {
+		/* Set updated compat struct, use "notrunc" flavor in order to
+		 * support the increase of the struct by FW.
+		 * "notrunc" will not truncate the stored struct, even if
+		 * it's size is larger than 'size'.
+		 */
+		ret = hl_boot_info_set_notrunc(type, data, size);
+		if (ret)
+			LOG_ERR("%s: Failed in hl_boot_info_set_notrunc (%d)\n", __func__, ret);
+
+	} else {
+		/* Mismatched version - reset compat struct.
+		 * Changes in version indicates a non-compatible change
+		 * to the compat struct. This make stored struct irrelevant.
+		 */
+		*ver = curr_ver;
+		ret = hl_boot_info_set(type, data, size);
+		if (ret)
+			LOG_ERR("%s: Failed in hl_boot_info_set (%d)\n", __func__, ret);
+	}
+}
+
+void fw_info_set(void)
+{
+	compat_struct_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_INFO, &fw_info, sizeof(fw_info),
+				HL_FW_INFO_VERSION, fw_info_get_ver);
+}
+
+void mem_info_set(void)
+{
+	compat_struct_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_MEM_INFO, &mem_info, sizeof(mem_info),
+				HL_MEM_INFO_VERSION, mem_info_get_ver);
+}
+
+static int compat_struct_init(enum hl_boot_info_type type, void *data, size_t size,
+			uint32_t curr_ver, get_ver_cb get_ver, u32 *ver)
+{
+	uint32_t *stored_ver;
+	int err;
+
+	/* Using copy_partial allows stored struct to be in different size than
+	 * allocated struct size. This is imperative for forward and back
+	 * compatibility.
+	 */
+	err = hl_boot_info_copy_partial(type, data, 0, size);
+	if (err)
+		return -EEXIST;
+
+	stored_ver = get_ver(data);
+	if (!stored_ver)
+		return -EFAULT;
+
+	*ver = *stored_ver;
+	if (*stored_ver == curr_ver)
+		return 0;
+
+	/* ignoring stored data */
+	memset(data, 0, size);
+
+	return -EFAULT;
+}
+
+static void compat_struct_init_print_err(const char *name, int err, uint32_t ver)
+{
+	switch (err) {
+	case -EFAULT:
+		LOG_WRN("Ignoring %s with version: 0x%08x", name, ver);
+		break;
+	case -EEXIST:
+		/* Do nothing, fw_info may not exists in some cases
+		 * (e.g. after cold boot)
+		 */
+		break;
+	default:
+		LOG_ERR("%s unknown init error: %d", name, err);
+		break;
+	}
+}
+
+void fw_info_init(void)
+{
+	uint32_t ver = INVALID_VERSION;
+	int err;
+
+	err = compat_struct_init(HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_INFO, &fw_info, sizeof(fw_info),
+					HL_FW_INFO_VERSION, fw_info_get_ver, &ver);
+	if (err)
+		compat_struct_init_print_err("fw_info", err, ver);
+}
+
+void mem_info_init(void)
+{
+	uint32_t ver = INVALID_VERSION;
+	int err;
+
+	err = compat_struct_init(HL_BOOT_INFO_MEM_INFO, &mem_info, sizeof(mem_info),
+					HL_MEM_INFO_VERSION, mem_info_get_ver, &ver);
+	if (err)
+		compat_struct_init_print_err("mem_info", err, ver);
+}

b/lib/common/memory.c

@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2021 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <util.h>
+#include <memory.h>
+#include <memory_dev.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(LIB_MEMORY_ID);
+
+struct sys_heap mem_heap_main;
+
+int heap_init(struct sys_heap *heap, void *base, u32 size)
+{
+	sys_heap_init(heap, base, size);
+	if (!sys_heap_validate(heap)) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Telem Heap init failed at addr 0x%x", __func__, (u32)base);
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+__weak u64 preboot_translate_addr(u64 addr)
+{
+	return addr;
+}
+
+static int main_heap_init(void)
+{
+	return heap_init(&mem_heap_main, (void *)MEM_HEAP_BASE, MEM_HEAP_SZ);
+}
+
+__weak int dev_spec_heap_init(void) { return 0; }
+
+int preboot_heap_init(void)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = main_heap_init();
+	err |= dev_spec_heap_init();
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+u64 preboot_axi2local_translate(u64 addr)
+{
+	return preboot_translate_addr(addr);
+}

b/lib/common/module_pci.c

@@ -0,0 +1,242 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright 2016-2020 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+
+#include <util.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <hl_device.h>
+#include <shutdown.h>
+#include <watchdog_timer.h>
+#include <hl_boot_info.h>
+#include <common_utils.h>
+#include <common.h>
+
+#include "cmd_module.h"
+#include "pci.h"
+#include "pll.h"
+#include <hl.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(module_pci);
+
+static void module_pci_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3);
+static void module_pci_run_noret(void);
+
+static struct pci_status pci_sts = {
+	.flr_reg_val = ~1,
+	.hot_rst_reg_val = ~1,
+	.smlh_link_reg_val = 1,
+	.rdlh_link_reg_val = 1,
+	.link_is_up = false,
+	.mem_enabled_prev = false,
+	.pci_pll_initialized = false,
+	.setup_done = false,
+	.wdog_en = false,
+	.pif_err_pending = false,
+	.thread_can_run = true,
+	.mstr_en = false,
+	.mstr_en_prev = false,
+};
+
+#define MODULE_PCI_STACKSIZE		(1024 - CONFIG_HL_STACK_OVERFLOW_REDUCE_SIZE)
+#define PCI_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC	K_MSEC(1000)
+static k_tid_t thread_id;
+static struct k_thread module_pci_thread;
+static struct wdt_thread_desc *thread_desc;
+static K_THREAD_STACK_DEFINE(module_pci_stack, MODULE_PCI_STACKSIZE);
+
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_setup(void) {}
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_shutdown(void) {}
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_run_noret(void) {}
+
+#define PCIE_AUX_FLR_CTRL_HW_CTRL_MASK	0x1
+#define PCIE_AUX_FLR_CTRL_INT_MASK_MASK	0x2
+
+__weak void pci_dev_flr_enable(bool enable)
+{
+	u32 val = enable ? 0 : PCIE_AUX_FLR_CTRL_HW_CTRL_MASK |
+				PCIE_AUX_FLR_CTRL_INT_MASK_MASK;
+
+	regmap_writel(val, pci.aux_flr_ctrl_reg);
+}
+
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_hot_rst(void)
+{
+	regmap_writel(0x10, pci.core_hot_rst_reg);
+	regmap_writel(1, pci.aux_ltssm_en_reg);
+}
+
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_flr(void) {}
+__weak void hl_pci_iatu_init(void) {}
+__weak void hl_pci_bmu_init(void) {}
+__weak bool is_pcie_initialized(void) { return true; }
+__weak void hl_dev_pci_pll_wa(void) {}
+__weak bool pci_dev_specific_no_ret_check(void) { return false; }
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_post_link_up(void) {}
+__weak void pci_dev_specific_pif_err_process(void) {}
+__weak bool pci_dev_specific_pif_err_pending(void) { return false; }
+__weak void preboot_print_ltssm_info(void) {}
+__weak void preboot_inband_telemetry_init(void) {}
+
+
+k_tid_t module_pci_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	if (is_pcie_initialized()) {
+		module_pci_do_common_setup(&pci_sts);
+		preboot_inband_telemetry_init();
+	}
+
+	if (hl_rst_src_get() == HL_COLD_RST)
+		preboot_print_ltssm_info();
+
+	thread_id = k_thread_create(&module_pci_thread, module_pci_stack, MODULE_PCI_STACKSIZE,
+				    module_pci_check, NULL, NULL, NULL, CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY,
+				    K_INHERIT_PERMS, K_NO_WAIT);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(&module_pci_thread, thread_name);
+
+	return thread_id;
+}
+
+__weak u32 pci_dev_specific_hot_rst_check(void)
+{
+	return regmap_readl(pci.core_hot_rst_reg) & BIT(0);
+}
+
+__weak void dev_specific_module_pci_check(void)
+{
+	unsigned int rst_src = hl_rst_src_get();
+
+	/*
+	 * don't read pci status regs until PRSTn is deasserted and pci blocks
+	 * are functional
+	 */
+	if (!is_pcie_initialized()) {
+		pci_init_wait_dbg();
+
+		/* process PIF ARC errors before link up, this is safe when prst_n is asserted */
+		if (pci_dev_specific_pif_err_pending())
+			pci_dev_specific_pif_err_process();
+
+		return;
+	}
+
+	if (!pci_sts.setup_done) {
+		module_pci_do_common_setup(&pci_sts);
+		preboot_inband_telemetry_init();
+	}
+
+	/* Check if FOUND_NO_RETURN to be returned such that statemachine calls run_noreturn() to
+	 * trigger reset.
+	 */
+	if (pci_dev_specific_no_ret_check()) {
+		/* terminate all other thread cmd modules */
+		cmd_module_shutdown(thread_id, SHUTDOWN_MODE_SET_RESET_TIMEOUT);
+
+		module_pci_run_noret();
+
+		return;
+	}
+
+	pci_sts.flr_reg_val = regmap_readl(pci.aux_flr_int_reg);
+	pci_sts.hot_rst_reg_val = pci_dev_specific_hot_rst_check();
+	pci_sts.rdlh_link_reg_val = regmap_readl(pci.aux_rdlh_link_up_reg);
+	pci_sts.smlh_link_reg_val = regmap_readl(pci.aux_smlh_link_up_reg);
+	pci_sts.pif_err_pending = pci_dev_specific_pif_err_pending();
+	pci_sts.mem_enabled = hl_pci_is_mem_enabled();
+
+	if (!pci_sts.pci_pll_initialized || !pci_sts.wdog_en) {
+		if (!pci_sts.pci_pll_initialized) {
+			if ((rst_src == HL_COLD_RST) || (rst_src == HL_PRSTN_RST))
+				hl_dev_pci_pll_wa();
+
+			pci_sts.pci_pll_initialized = true;
+		}
+
+		if (!pci_sts.wdog_en) {
+			if (!wdt_is_initialized())
+				watchdog_init(WATCHDOG_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS);
+			pci_sts.wdog_en = true;
+		}
+
+		return;
+	}
+
+	module_pci_do_common_run(&pci_sts);
+
+	if (pci_sts.pif_err_pending)
+		pci_dev_specific_pif_err_process();
+}
+
+static void module_pci_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3)
+{
+	while (pci_sts.thread_can_run) {
+		if (wdt_is_initialized() &&
+			watchdog_watch_threads() &&
+			thread_desc == NULL)
+			thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+		if (hl_is_debug_mode(HL_DEBUG_HW_PCIE))
+			watchdog_yield();
+
+		if (thread_desc)
+			wdt_thread_keep_alive(thread_desc);
+
+		dev_specific_module_pci_check();
+
+		watchdog_yield();
+	}
+
+	if (!hl_is_debug_mode(HL_DEBUG_HW_PCIE))
+		pci_dev_specific_shutdown();
+
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+
+	pci_sts.setup_done = false;
+}
+
+void module_pci_shutdown(void)
+{
+	if (!is_pcie_initialized())
+		return;
+
+	pci_sts.thread_can_run = false;
+	k_thread_join(thread_id, PCI_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC);
+}
+
+static void module_pci_preserve_sp_info(void)
+{
+	int ret;
+
+	preboot_finalize_fw_info();
+	preboot_finalize_nic_en();
+
+	ret = hl_boot_info_finalize();
+	if (ret)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: failed in module_lkd_preserve_sp_info, ret=%d", __func__, ret);
+}
+
+static void module_pci_run_noret(void)
+{
+	preboot_if_shutdown();
+	module_pci_preserve_sp_info();
+	pci_dev_specific_run_noret();
+}
+
+bool hl_pci_link_is_up(void)
+{
+	return pci_sts.link_is_up;
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_pci_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return thread_id;
+}

b/lib/common/thermal.c

@@ -0,0 +1,282 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+#include <zephyr.h>
+#include <stdbool.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <efuse.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+#include "thermal.h"
+
+#define HL_TS_SDIF_DATA_OFFSET	0x44
+#define HL_TS_CLK_SYNTH		0x010
+#define HL_TS_SDIF		0x020
+#define HL_TS0_ALARMA_CFG	0x144
+#define HL_TS0_ALARMB_CFG	0x164
+#define C_TO_MC			1000
+
+#define TS_CALIB_J_COEFF        1
+#define TS_CALIB_DIV_COEFF      4094
+#define TS_CALIB_FCLK_COEFF     106
+#define TS_CALIB_OPMODE_COEFF   0x0
+
+#define TS_UNCALIB_J_COEFF      16
+#define TS_UNCALIB_DIV_COEFF    4094
+#define TS_UNCALIB_FCLK_COEFF   5
+#define TS_UNCALIB_OPMODE_COEFF 0x1
+
+struct hl_ts_cfg hl_ts_data[HL_ASIC_TS_MAX_COUNT];
+
+__weak void hl_ts_clk_set(u32 addr, unsigned int clk)
+{
+	unsigned int synth_clk;
+	unsigned int val;
+
+	synth_clk = ((clk / 6000000) / 2) - 1;
+
+	val = synth_clk;
+	val |= synth_clk << 8;
+	val |= 0x101 << 16;
+	regmap_writel(val, addr + HL_TS_CLK_SYNTH);
+
+}
+
+__weak void hl_ts_sdif_set(u32 addr, bool use_calib)
+{
+	k_busy_wait(100);
+	/* program sensor power up delay */
+	regmap_writel(0x8d0000ff, addr + HL_TS_SDIF);
+	k_busy_wait(100);
+	/* configure sensor mode */
+	regmap_writel(0x89000000, addr + HL_TS_SDIF);
+	k_busy_wait(100);
+	/* power up sensor in continuous mode*/
+	regmap_writel(0x88000108, addr + HL_TS_SDIF);
+}
+
+/* convert 12-bit HL_TMP register value to milliCelsius */
+__weak long hl_tmp_temp_reg_to_mC(int val, int ts)
+{
+	long tmp = (val & 0xfff);
+	u32 coef_a_g, coef_b_h, coef_j, coef_div, fclk;
+
+	coef_a_g = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_a_g;
+	coef_b_h = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_b_h;
+	coef_div = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_div;
+	coef_j = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_j;
+	fclk = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.fclk;
+
+	tmp = coef_a_g + ((coef_b_h * (((tmp * 1000) / coef_div) -
+					500)) / 1000) - coef_j * fclk;
+
+	/*
+	 * result of equation has to be multiplied
+	 * by 1000, since coefficients are multiplied by 100 an extra
+	 * multiplication of 10 is required.
+	 */
+	tmp *= 10;
+
+	return tmp;
+}
+
+/* convert milliCelsius to 12-bit HL_TMP register value */
+__weak u32 hl_tmp_mC_to_temp_reg(long val, int ts)
+{
+	u32 reg_val;
+	u32 coef_a_g, coef_b_h, coef_j, coef_div, fclk;
+
+	coef_a_g = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_a_g;
+	coef_b_h = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_b_h;
+	coef_div = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_div;
+	coef_j = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_j;
+	fclk = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.fclk;
+
+	reg_val = ((coef_div * ((val / 10) -
+			coef_a_g + (coef_j * fclk) +
+			(coef_b_h / 2))) / coef_b_h);
+
+	return reg_val & 0xfff;
+}
+
+/* twos complement to u32 without changing result sign
+ * since the temperature formula uses the positive value of
+ * the conversion.
+ */
+static inline s32 twos_complement_to_s32(u32 val)
+{
+	return  val & 0x8000 ? ((~val & 0xFFFF) + 1) * (-1) : val;
+}
+
+/* Extract A and B coefficients from efuse
+ *
+ * A:
+ * N_bin_twos_comp = efuse_read & 0x0000FFFF
+ * N_bin = twos_comp(N_bin_twos_comp, 16) #twos complement to int
+ * A_North = (N_bin * (-1)) / 1000.0
+ * A = A + A_North
+ *
+ * B:
+ * N_bin_twos_comp = (efuse_read >> (2) * 8) & 0x0000FFFF
+ * N_bin = twos_comp(N_bin_twos_comp, 16) #twos complement to int
+ * B_North = (N_bin * (-1)) / 1000.0
+ * B = B + B_North
+ */
+static inline void hl_tmp_extract_A_B(u32 calib_data, int ts)
+{
+	s32 efuse_a_coeff = calib_data & 0xFFFF;
+	s32 efuse_b_coeff = (calib_data >> 16) & 0xFFFF;
+
+	/* convert from two's complement to int */
+	efuse_a_coeff = twos_complement_to_s32(efuse_a_coeff);
+	efuse_b_coeff = twos_complement_to_s32(efuse_b_coeff);
+
+	/* A and B extracted has to be divided by 1000,
+	 * but since defined coefficients are multiplied by 100
+	 * for accuracy purposes, the coeff extracted are divided
+	 * only by 10 to match defined coefficients resolution.
+	 */
+	hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_a_g += efuse_a_coeff / 10;
+	hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_b_h += efuse_b_coeff / 10;
+}
+
+__weak int get_alarma_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS0_ALARMA_CFG;
+}
+
+__weak int get_alarmb_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS0_ALARMB_CFG;
+}
+
+static inline void hl_ts_alarm_set(u32 ts_num)
+{
+	long reg;
+	int alarm_mc;
+
+	alarm_mc = hl_ts_data[ts_num].alarm_a * C_TO_MC;
+	reg = hl_tmp_mC_to_temp_reg(alarm_mc, ts_num);
+	regmap_writel(reg, hl_ts_data[ts_num].base + get_alarma_offset());
+
+	alarm_mc = hl_ts_data[ts_num].alarm_b * C_TO_MC;
+	reg = hl_tmp_mC_to_temp_reg(alarm_mc, ts_num);
+	regmap_writel(reg, hl_ts_data[ts_num].base + get_alarmb_offset());
+}
+
+__weak void hl_set_calib_coeff(int ts, bool calib_enabled,
+		struct hl_tmp_opmode_info *calib_data)
+{
+	if (calib_enabled) {
+		calib_data->coef_a_g = TS_CALIB_A_G_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_b_h = TS_CALIB_B_H_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_j = TS_CALIB_J_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_div = TS_CALIB_DIV_COEFF;
+		calib_data->fclk = TS_CALIB_FCLK_COEFF;
+		calib_data->opmode = TS_CALIB_OPMODE_COEFF;
+	} else {
+		calib_data->coef_a_g = TS_UNCALIB_A_G_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_b_h = TS_UNCALIB_B_H_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_j = TS_UNCALIB_J_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_div = TS_UNCALIB_DIV_COEFF;
+		calib_data->fclk = TS_UNCALIB_FCLK_COEFF;
+		calib_data->opmode = TS_UNCALIB_OPMODE_COEFF;
+	}
+
+}
+
+__weak int hl_ts_get_count(void)
+{
+	return HL_ASIC_TS_MAX_COUNT;
+}
+
+__weak int get_sdif_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS_SDIF;
+}
+
+__weak int hl_ts_pvt_init(int ts)
+{
+	return 0;
+}
+
+__weak void hl_ts_data_init(void)
+{
+	memset(hl_ts_data, 0, sizeof(struct hl_ts_cfg) * HL_ASIC_TS_MAX_COUNT);
+}
+
+__weak void hl_dev_ts_init(void)
+{
+	u32 efuse_word, ts;
+
+	hl_ts_data_init();
+
+	for (ts = 0; ts < hl_ts_get_count(); ts++) {
+
+		if (hl_ts_pvt_init(ts))
+			continue;
+
+		if (!hl_ts_data[ts].use_calib)
+			hl_set_calib_coeff(ts, false,
+						&hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data);
+
+		else {
+			if (preboot_fuse_read(hl_ts_data[ts].calib_offset,
+						&efuse_word))
+				return;
+
+			hl_set_calib_coeff(ts, true,
+						&hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data);
+			/* Calculate new A and B coeff using efuse offsets */
+			hl_tmp_extract_A_B(efuse_word, ts);
+		}
+
+		hl_ts_alarm_set(ts);
+		hl_ts_clk_set(hl_ts_data[ts].base, hl_ts_data[ts].freq);
+		hl_ts_sdif_set(hl_ts_data[ts].base, hl_ts_data[ts].use_calib);
+
+	}
+}
+
+void hl_ts_init(void)
+{
+	hl_dev_ts_init();
+}
+
+__weak void hl_ts_pvt_shutdown(int ts) {}
+__weak void hl_ts_reset(void) {}
+
+__weak void hl_ts_shutdown(void)
+{
+	u32 ts;
+
+	for (ts = 0; ts < hl_ts_get_count(); ts++)
+		hl_ts_pvt_shutdown(ts);
+
+	hl_ts_reset();
+}
+
+__weak int get_sdif_data_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS_SDIF_DATA_OFFSET;
+}
+
+__weak int hl_ts_get_max_temp(void)
+{
+	int tmp, max_tmp = 0;
+	int ts_num, ts;
+
+	ts_num = hl_ts_get_count();
+	for (ts = 0; ts < ts_num; ts++) {
+		tmp = regmap_readl(hl_ts_data[ts].base +
+					get_sdif_data_offset());
+		tmp = hl_tmp_temp_reg_to_mC(tmp, ts);
+		if (max_tmp < tmp)
+			max_tmp = tmp;
+	}
+
+	return max_tmp;
+}

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0 */

b/lib/common/thermal_v2.c

@@ -0,0 +1,234 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2017-2020 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <zephyr.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+#include "thermal.h"
+#include "common.h"
+#include <tstd.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(thermal_v2);
+
+#define HL_TS_IP_CTRL				0x00
+#define HL_TS_IP_CTRL_AUTO_CONT_MODE		0x108
+#define HL_TS_IP_CTRL_RUN_ONCE			0x4
+#define HL_TS_IP_CFG				0x01
+#define HL_TS_IP_TMR				0x05
+#define HL_TS_IP_TMR_DELAY			0xff
+
+/*
+ * Synthetic clock should be between 4MHz and 8MHz (Use 6MHz)
+ * TODO: confirm clock rate.
+ */
+#define HL_TMP_CLK_RATE				6000000
+
+/*
+ * Note that coefficients are multiplied by 100 for better
+ * accuracy in result.
+ */
+#define TS_UNCALIB_DIV_COEFF			4096
+#define TS_CALIB_DIV_COEFF			4096
+
+#define HL_PVT_COMP_ID				0x00
+#define HL_PVT_COMP_ID_VALUE			0x9b487062
+#define HL_PVT_TM_SCRATCH			0x0c
+#define HL_PVT_TM_SCRATCH_PATTERN		0xAAAAAAAA
+#define HL_TS_PVT_INIT_FAILED			0x1
+#define HL_TS_IP_CTRL_SHUTDOWN			1
+
+void hl_set_calib_coeff(int ts, bool calib_enabled,
+		struct hl_tmp_opmode_info *calib_data)
+{
+	if (calib_enabled) {
+		calib_data->coef_a_g = TS_CALIB_A_G_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_b_h = TS_CALIB_B_H_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_div = TS_CALIB_DIV_COEFF;
+		calib_data->opmode = true;
+	} else {
+		calib_data->coef_a_g = TS_UNCALIB_A_G_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_b_h = TS_UNCALIB_B_H_COEFF;
+		calib_data->coef_div = TS_UNCALIB_DIV_COEFF;
+		calib_data->opmode = false;
+	}
+}
+
+static bool hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(u32 addr)
+{
+	unsigned int regval;
+	int retry = 5;
+
+	do {
+		regval = regmap_readl(addr + HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_STATUS);
+		if ((regval & HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_STATUS_BUSY_MASK) == 0)
+			return true;
+
+		retry--;
+		k_busy_wait(100);
+	} while (retry != 0);
+
+	return false;
+}
+
+static inline void sdif_write(u32 reg_add, u32 wdata, u8 addr,
+		bool wrn, bool prog)
+{
+
+	u32 regval = (wdata << HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_WDATA_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_WDATA_MASK;
+	regval |= (addr << HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_ADDR_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_ADDR_MASK;
+	regval |= (wrn << HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_WRN_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_WRN_MASK;
+	regval |= (prog << HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_PROG_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_SDIF_PROG_MASK;
+
+	regmap_writel(regval, reg_add + HL_TS_CMN_SDIF);
+}
+
+void hl_ts_sdif_set(u32 addr, bool use_calib)
+{
+	/* program sensor power up delay */
+	if (hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(addr))
+		sdif_write(addr, HL_TS_IP_TMR_DELAY, HL_TS_IP_TMR, 1, 1);
+
+	/* configure sensor mode */
+	if (hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(addr))
+		sdif_write(addr, use_calib, HL_TS_IP_CFG, 1, 1);
+
+	/* power up sensor in continuous mode */
+	if (hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(addr))
+		sdif_write(addr, HL_TS_IP_CTRL_AUTO_CONT_MODE, HL_TS_IP_CTRL,
+				1, 1);
+}
+
+void hl_ts_clk_set(u32 addr, unsigned int clk)
+{
+	unsigned int synth_clk;
+	unsigned int val;
+
+	synth_clk = ((clk / HL_TMP_CLK_RATE) / 2) - 1;
+
+	val = (synth_clk << HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_LO_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_LO_MASK;
+	val |= (synth_clk << HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_HI_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_HI_MASK;
+	val |= (1 << HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_HOLD_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_HOLD_MASK;
+	val |= (1 << HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_ENA_SHIFT)
+		& HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH_ENA_MASK;
+
+	regmap_writel(val, addr + HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH);
+}
+
+/*
+ * convert 12-bit HL_TMP register value to milliCelsius
+ *
+ * Mode 1: output conversion (calibrated)
+ * Temp = A + B * Eqbs
+ *
+ * Mode 2: output conversion (uncalibrated)
+ * Temp = G + H * Eqbs
+ *
+ * Eqbs = Nbs / cal5 - 0.5
+ *
+ */
+
+long hl_tmp_temp_reg_to_mC(int val, int ts)
+{
+	s64 tmp = (val & HL_TS_00_SDIF_DATA_SMPL_DATA_MASK);
+	s32 coef_a_g, coef_b_h, coef_div;
+
+	coef_a_g = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_a_g;
+	coef_b_h = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_b_h;
+	coef_div = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_div;
+
+	tmp = coef_a_g + ((coef_b_h * (((tmp * 1000) / coef_div) -
+					500)) / 1000);
+
+	return tmp;
+}
+
+/* convert milliCelsius to 12-bit HL_TMP register value */
+u32 hl_tmp_mC_to_temp_reg(long val, int ts)
+{
+	u32 reg_val;
+	s32 coef_a_g, coef_b_h, coef_div;
+
+	coef_a_g = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_a_g;
+	coef_b_h = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_b_h;
+	coef_div = hl_ts_data[ts].hl_tmp_data.coef_div;
+
+	reg_val = ((coef_div * ((val / 10) -
+					coef_a_g + (coef_b_h / 2))) / coef_b_h);
+
+	return reg_val & HL_TS_00_SDIF_DATA_SMPL_DATA_MASK;
+}
+
+int get_alarma_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS_00_ALARMA_CFG;
+}
+
+int get_alarmb_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS_00_ALARMB_CFG;
+}
+
+int get_sdif_data_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS_00_SDIF_DATA;
+}
+
+int get_sdif_offset(void)
+{
+	return HL_TS_CMN_SDIF;
+}
+
+int hl_ts_pvt_init(int ts)
+{
+	unsigned int regval;
+
+	regval = regmap_readl(hl_ts_data[ts].base + HL_PVT_COMP_ID);
+	if (regval !=  HL_PVT_COMP_ID_VALUE)
+		return HL_TS_PVT_INIT_FAILED;
+
+	regmap_writel(HL_PVT_TM_SCRATCH_PATTERN,
+			(hl_ts_data[ts].base + HL_PVT_TM_SCRATCH));
+	regval = regmap_readl(hl_ts_data[ts].base + HL_PVT_TM_SCRATCH);
+	if (regval !=  HL_PVT_TM_SCRATCH_PATTERN)
+		return HL_TS_PVT_INIT_FAILED;
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+void hl_ts_pvt_shutdown(int ts)
+{
+	u32 addr = hl_ts_data[ts].base;
+
+	/* Exit the continuous mode of operation */
+	if (hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(addr))
+		sdif_write(addr, HL_TS_IP_CTRL_RUN_ONCE, HL_TS_IP_CTRL, 1, 1);
+	else
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to exit continuous mode for %d", ts);
+
+	/* Ensure embedded IP is shut down ip_ctrl = 0x000001 */
+	if (hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(addr))
+		sdif_write(addr, HL_TS_IP_CTRL_SHUTDOWN, HL_TS_IP_CTRL, 1, 1);
+	else
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to shutdown TS IP %d", ts);
+
+	/* Disable TS data acquisitions and ensure clock is shutdown fully */
+	if (hl_tmp_is_sdif_free(addr))
+		regmap_writel(0, hl_ts_data[ts].base + HL_TS_CMN_CLK_SYNTH);
+	else
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to disable TS data acquisitions for %d", ts);
+}

+#define FWUPD_NORET_DELAY_US	200000

+static void fwupd_sync_exec(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	if (state->sync_exec_res == NOT_FOUND || !state->noret_delay)
+		return;
+
+	if (timer_expired(state->noret_delay, FWUPD_NORET_DELAY_US)) {
+		LOG_WRN("firing no ret now!");
+		state->exec_res = state->sync_exec_res;
+		state->sync_exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+	}
+}
+
+static void fwupd_get_proto_status(struct hl_upd_proto_status *status,
+				   int err, int serr)
+{
+	status->version = HL_UPD_PROTO_VERSION;
+	status->compat_mask = cpu_boot_dev_sts_get_all();
+	status->fw_comp = HL_FW_COMP_PREBOOT;
+	status->err = err;
+	status->sub_err = serr;
+}
+
+static int fwupd_create_resp(void *res_fit, u32 avail_sz, const int load_type,
+			     const struct fwupd_resp_prop *props, int prop_cnt, u32 *out_sz)
+{
+	int err, i;
+	unsigned int sz;
+
+	err = fit_init_resp(res_fit, avail_sz);
+	if (err)
+		goto fit_init_err;
+
+	err = fdt_property_u32(res_fit, HL_FIT_LOAD_TYPE_PROP, load_type);
+	if (err)
+		goto fit_init_err;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < prop_cnt; i++) {
+		err = fdt_property(res_fit, props[i].name, props[i].data, props[i].size);
+		if (err)
+			goto fit_init_err;
+	}
+
+	err = fit_fin_resp(res_fit, &sz);
+	if (err)
+		goto fit_init_err;
+
+	*out_sz = sz;
+
+fit_init_err:
+	return err;
+}

+__weak u32 preboot_get_elbi_reg_offset_from_base(uintptr_t address)
+{
+	LOG_WRN("%s is not implemented", __func__);

+}

+#define PROTO_STS_MAX_RES_SZ	SZ_4K

+static int module_fwupd_proto_status(struct fwupd_state *state,
+				     struct hl_upd_proto_status *ret_status)
+{
+	struct hl_upd_proto_status status;
+	struct fwupd_resp_prop *resp_prop;
+	u32 sts_err, sts_serr;
+	int err, final_size = 0;
+
+	resp_prop = &resp_props[RESP_PROP_STATUS];
+	resp_prop->name = HL_FIT_RES_PROTO_STS_PROP;
+	resp_prop->data = &status;
+	resp_prop->size = sizeof(status);
+
+	resp_prop = &resp_props[RESP_PROP_SPI_VERSION];
+	resp_prop->name = HL_FIT_RES_SPI_VERSION_PROP;
+	resp_prop->data = preboot_get_version(PREBOOT_BTL_VER);
+	resp_prop->size = VERSION_MAX_LEN;
+
+	/* if heap is already allocated, then use it */
+	if (!state->is_heap_alloc) {
+		state->map_offset = sys_heap_aligned_alloc(&mem_heap_main, SZ_4K,
+							   PROTO_STS_MAX_RES_SZ);
+		if (!state->map_offset) {
+			LOG_ERR("%s: Failed in alloc", __func__);
+			return 1;
+		}
+
+		state->is_heap_alloc = true;
+	}
+
+	/*
+	 * Here we assume that the error is reported only once and
+	 * regardless of the interface
+	 */
+	preboot_error_read_and_clr(&sts_err, &sts_serr);
+	fwupd_get_proto_status(&status, sts_err, sts_serr);
+
+	err = fwupd_create_resp(state->map_offset, PROTO_STS_MAX_RES_SZ, FIT_LOAD_TYPE_PROTO_STATUS,
+				resp_props, ARRAY_SIZE(resp_props), &final_size);
+
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to create status FIT image with err=%d", err);
+
+	if (ret_status)
+		*ret_status = status;
+
+	state->map_size = ROUND_UP(final_size, SZ_4K);
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+__weak u32 dev_get_fwupd_status_offset(uintptr_t address)
+{
+	LOG_WRN("%s is not implemented", __func__);
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_send_status(struct fwupd_state *state,
+				     enum hl_upd_sts sts, void *offset)
+{
+	struct hl_upd_status status;
+	uintptr_t address = (uintptr_t)offset;
+	int err;
+
+	switch (sts) {
+	case HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ERR:
+	case HL_UPD_STS_ERR:
+	case HL_UPD_STS_RST_ERR:
+	case HL_UPD_STS_VALID_ERR:
+	case HL_UPD_STS_TIMEOUT_ERR:
+		/*
+		 * The error inform function, work in such a way
+		 * that the error will be set only once (assuming no
+		 * concurrency) so if the error inform function is called before
+		 * entering the current function, module_fwupd_send_status,
+		 * the second call will not overrun its values.
+		 * it allows support legacy code by setting the sent error
+		 * status as protocol error and set it's minor value.
+		 */
+		preboot_error_inform(HL_FW_ERR_PROTO, sts);
+
+		/* if an explicit FIT was attached - use it */
+		if (!address) {
+			err = module_fwupd_proto_status(state, NULL);
+			/* if prepared ok - just include it */
+			if (!err)
+				address = (uintptr_t)state->map_offset;
+		}
+		/* no break */
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+
+	status.val = 0;
+	status.status = sts;
+
+	/* only if we get a address we need to calc offset */
+	if (state->wfc_cmd.cmd == HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI || state->wfc_cmd.cmd == HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI) {
+		if (address) {
+			status.offset = preboot_get_elbi_reg_offset_from_base(address);
+			if (state->use_security)
+				preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_open();
+		} else {
+			status.offset = 0;
+		}
+	} else {
+		status.offset = dev_get_fwupd_status_offset(address);
+
+		/* In case of MAP_SRAM, the access should be already open in RW mode
+		 * overrunning with RO, will not allow the host to transfer the FIT.
+		 */
+		if (state->use_security && status.offset && state->wfc_cmd.cmd != HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM)
+			preboot_security_dynamic_img_sram_open((uintptr_t)state->map_offset,
+							       state->map_size, ACCESS_RO);
+	}
+
+	state->sts_writel(status.val);
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_fwupd_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	state.cmd_readl = fwupd_cmd_readl;
+	state.sts_writel = fwupd_sts_writel;
+	state.use_security = true;
+	state.thread_can_run = true;
+	state.elbi_offset = FWUPD_ELBI_FIT_OFFSET_PCI;
+	module_fwupd_send_status(&state, HL_UPD_STS_RST_OK, NULL);
+
+	fwupd_tid = k_thread_create(&module_fwupd_thread, module_fwupd_stack, MODULE_FWUPD_STACKSIZE,
+				    module_fwupd_check, NULL, NULL, NULL, CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY, K_INHERIT_PERMS,
+				    K_NO_WAIT);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(&module_fwupd_thread, thread_name);
+
+	return fwupd_tid;
+}
+
+/* NOOP is a sync command, so here we decide what execution type is needed */
+static void module_fwupd_check_noop(struct fwupd_state *state, k_tid_t thread_id,
+				    void (*run)(void), void (*run_noreturn)(k_tid_t))
+{
+	/* check the command we got before waiting for sync */
+	switch (state->wfc_cmd.cmd) {
+	case HL_UPD_NOOP:
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RST_DEV:
+		run_noreturn(thread_id);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_SRAM:
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI:
+	case HL_UPD_GET_PROTO_STATUS:
+	case HL_UPD_RST_STATE:
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM:
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI:
+	default:
+		run();
+		break;
+	}
+}
+
+static void __module_fwupd_check(struct fwupd_state *state, k_tid_t thread_id,
+				 void (*run)(void), void (*run_noreturn)(k_tid_t thread_id))
+{
+	/*
+	 * the error status response, involved
+	 * the creation of a FIT file.
+	 * Due to the lack of commands numbers the cmd state (4 bits)
+	 * can't be change to a specific state therefore a flag was added
+	 * other option is to add an internal cmd level.
+	 */
+	fwupd_sync_exec(state);
+	if (state->exec_res != NOT_FOUND) {
+		switch (state->exec_res) {
+		case FOUND_RETURN:
+			run();
+			break;
+		case FOUND_NO_RETURN:
+			run_noreturn(thread_id);
+			return;
+		default:
+			break;
+		}
+	}
+
+	state->cmd.val = state->cmd_readl();
+
+	if (state->cmd.cmd == state->wfc_cmd.cmd)
+		return;
+
+	switch (state->cmd.cmd) {
+	case HL_UPD_NOOP:	/* NOOP is a special command */
+		module_fwupd_check_noop(state, thread_id, run, run_noreturn);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_CLR_STS:
+	case HL_UPD_RST_STATE:
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM:
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI:
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_SRAM:
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI:
+	case HL_UPD_GET_PROTO_STATUS:
+	case HL_UPD_RST_DEV:
+		run();
+		break;
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3)
+{
+	while (state.thread_can_run) {
+
+		if (wdt_is_initialized() &&
+			watchdog_watch_threads() &&
+			thread_desc == NULL)
+			thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+		if (!state.is_pcie_disabled) {
+			__module_fwupd_check(&state, fwupd_tid, module_fwupd_run,
+						module_fwupd_run_noret);
+		}
+
+		if (thread_desc)
+			wdt_thread_keep_alive(thread_desc);
+
+		watchdog_yield();
+	}
+
+
+	module_fwupd_dev_spec_shutdown();
+
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+
+	preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(false);
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_state_shift(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	/* do not shift if got CLR_STS several times */
+	if (state->cmd.cmd == HL_UPD_CLR_STS)
+		return;
+
+	/* save current command waiting for clear, until handled */
+	state->wfc_cmd = state->cmd;
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_internal_reset_state(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	if (state->is_heap_alloc && state->map_offset) {
+		memset(state->map_offset, 0, state->map_size);
+		sys_heap_free(&mem_heap_main, state->map_offset);
+	}
+
+	state->is_heap_alloc = false;
+	state->cmd.val = 0;	/* NOOP */
+	state->wfc_cmd.val = 0;	/* NOOP */
+	state->exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+	state->sync_exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+	state->map_size = 0;
+	state->map_offset = NULL;
+	state->noret_delay = 0;
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_reset_state(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	module_fwupd_internal_reset_state(state);
+	preboot_set_sram_sf_access(false);
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_sram_close();
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_close();
+}
+
+#define IB_FW_UPDATE_MASK	BIT(0)
+
+void module_fwupd_store_pcie_access(void)
+{
+	u32 fwupd_pcie_disable = state.is_pcie_disabled;
+	enum telem_status_id tsts;
+	int err;
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_UPD_CLOSURE, &fwupd_pcie_disable,
+				sizeof(fwupd_pcie_disable));
+	if (err)
+		goto store_pcie_access_error;
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_finalize();
+
+store_pcie_access_error:
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to store fwupd closure");
+		return;
+	}
+
+	tsts = telem_get_status();
+	if (tsts == TELEM_NOT_INITIALIZED)
+		return;
+
+	err = telem_data_set_partial_and_valid(TELEM_IMG_TYPE_SYS_STAT, &fwupd_pcie_disable,
+					       OFFSET_SIZE(struct t_sys_stat_packet,
+					       ib_fw_update_stat), IB_FW_UPDATE_MASK,
+					       IB_FW_UPDATE_MASK);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to set pci disable in ib sys state packet (%d)", err);
+}
+
+__weak bool fwupd_pcie_access_is_disabled(void)
+{
+	return false;
+}
+
+void module_fwupd_pcie_access_reload(void)
+{
+	uint32_t pcie_disable;
+	int err;
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_copy(HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_UPD_CLOSURE, &pcie_disable, sizeof(pcie_disable));
+	if (err) {
+		pcie_disable = fwupd_pcie_access_is_disabled();
+		LOG_INF("fwupd via pcie is reset to default (%s)", pcie_disable ? "disabled" :
+			"enabled");
+
+	} else {
+		LOG_INF("fwupd via pcie is %s", pcie_disable ? "disabled" : "enabled");
+	}
+
+	module_fwupd_pcie_disable(pcie_disable);
+	module_fwupd_store_pcie_access();
+}
+
+void module_fwupd_pcie_disable(bool is_pcie_disabled)
+{
+	state.is_pcie_disabled = is_pcie_disabled;
+}
+
+bool module_fwupd_is_pcie_disabled(void)
+{
+	return state.is_pcie_disabled;
+}
+
+static int module_fwupd_get_elbi_fit_base(struct fwupd_state *state, uintptr_t *base_offset)
+{
+	uintptr_t elbi_base;
+	int err;
+
+	err = preboot_get_elbi_reg_base(&elbi_base);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("Could not map ELBI FIT base: not supported for device");
+		return -1;
+	}
+
+	*base_offset = elbi_base + state->elbi_offset;
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static enum hl_upd_sts module_fwupd_map(struct fwupd_state *state,
+					unsigned int req_size, enum hl_upd_cmd cmd)
+{
+	int err;
+	enum hl_upd_sts sts = HL_UPD_STS_MAP_OK;
+	uintptr_t base_offset;
+	int size = ROUND_UP(req_size, SZ_4K);
+
+	if (cmd == HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI) {
+		err = preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(true);
+		if (err)
+			return HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ERR;
+
+		err = module_fwupd_get_elbi_fit_base(state, &base_offset);
+		if (err)
+			return HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ERR;
+	} else {
+		base_offset = (uintptr_t)sys_heap_aligned_alloc(&mem_heap_main, SZ_4K, size);
+		if (!base_offset) {
+			LOG_ERR("%s: Failed in alloc\n", __func__);
+			return HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ERR;
+		}
+
+		state->is_heap_alloc = true;
+	}
+
+	err = !preboot_is_valid_address_range(base_offset, size);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("Map address range is invalid");
+		return HL_UPD_STS_VALID_ERR;
+	}
+
+	state->map_offset = (void *)base_offset;
+	state->map_size = size;
+
+	if (state->use_security) {
+		if (cmd == HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM)
+			preboot_security_dynamic_img_sram_open(base_offset, size, ACCESS_RW);
+		else
+			preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_open();
+	}
+
+	return sts;
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_load_type_run(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	state->exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+
+	if (state->synced_no_return) {
+		state->sync_exec_res = FOUND_NO_RETURN;
+		state->noret_delay = timer_get_us();
+	} else {
+		state->exec_res = FOUND_NO_RETURN;
+	}
+}
+
+static enum hl_upd_sts module_fwupd_run_cmd(struct fwupd_state *state, enum hl_upd_cmd cmd,
+					    enum hl_upd_sts *status)
+{
+	int err;
+	enum hl_upd_sts sts = HL_UPD_STS_OK;
+	bool is_secured, is_run = false;
+	struct fit_error fit_err = {};
+
+	/*
+	 * we do not allow mapping of SRAM and SPSRAM at the same time -
+	 * close both.
+	 */
+	preboot_set_sram_sf_access(false);
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_sram_close();
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_close();
+
+	is_secured = state->use_security && preboot_security_is_enabled();
+	err = fit_verify(state->map_offset, &fit_err);
+	if (!err)
+		err = fit_load_type(state->map_offset, is_secured, &is_run, &fit_err);
+
+	fit_report_error(&fit_err);
+	if (is_run)
+		module_fwupd_load_type_run(state);
+
+	if (cmd == HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI)
+		preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(false);
+
+	if (err) {
+		sts = HL_UPD_STS_ERR;
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to load FIT image");
+	}
+
+	*status = sts;
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_run_noop(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	enum hl_upd_sts sts = HL_UPD_STS_NOOP;
+	int err;
+
+	/* depending on the current state machine, perform action */
+	switch (state->wfc_cmd.cmd) {
+	case HL_UPD_NOOP:
+	case HL_UPD_CLR_STS:
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RST_STATE:
+		module_fwupd_reset_state(state);
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_RST_OK, NULL);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM:
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI:
+		sts = module_fwupd_map(state, state->wfc_cmd.size, state->wfc_cmd.cmd);
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, sts, state->map_offset);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_SRAM:
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI:
+		if (watchdog_watch_threads())
+			wdt_start_time_limited_section(WATCHDOG_LOAD_FIT_TIMEOUT_MS);
+
+		err = module_fwupd_run_cmd(state, state->wfc_cmd.cmd, &sts);
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, sts, NULL);
+		if (watchdog_watch_threads())
+			wdt_stop_time_limited_section();
+
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_GET_PROTO_STATUS:
+		err = module_fwupd_proto_status(state, NULL);
+		if (err)
+			module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_ERR, NULL);
+		else
+			module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_OK, state->map_offset);
+		break;
+	default:	/* should never get here */
+		LOG_ERR("%s: default case - err", __func__);
+	}
+}
+
+static void __module_fwupd_run(struct fwupd_state *state)
+{
+	const char *cmd_str = NULL;
+	const char *err_str = NULL;
+	enum hl_upd_sts sts = HL_UPD_STS_NOOP;
+	int err = 0;
+
+	state->run_mode = FOUND_RETURN;
+
+	switch (state->cmd.cmd) {
+	case HL_UPD_NOOP:	/* NOOP is a special command */
+		cmd_str = "noop";
+		module_fwupd_run_noop(state);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_CLR_STS:
+		cmd_str = "clr_sts";
+		/* Clear the status */
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_NOOP, NULL);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RST_STATE:
+		cmd_str = "rst_state";
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_RST_ACK, NULL);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM:
+		cmd_str = "map_sram";
+		/* no break */
+	case HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "map_elbi";
+		/* Protect from multi-MAP */
+		if (state->map_offset || state->map_size) {
+			sts = HL_UPD_STS_VALID_ERR;
+			err_str = "MAP is already in progress...";
+			err = 1;
+		} else {
+			sts = HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ACK;
+		}
+
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, sts, NULL);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_SRAM:
+		cmd_str = "run_sram";
+		/* no break */
+	case HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "run_elbi";
+		/* Protect from RUN before MAP */
+		if (state->map_offset && state->map_size) {
+			sts = HL_UPD_STS_ACK;
+		} else {
+			sts = HL_UPD_STS_VALID_ERR;
+			err_str = "RUN is not allowed before MAP...";
+			err = 1;
+		}
+
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, sts, NULL);
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_GET_PROTO_STATUS:
+		cmd_str = "proto_status";
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_ACK, NULL);
+		/* We ACK the command, but handle only on CLR, so save cmd */
+		break;
+	case HL_UPD_RST_DEV:
+		cmd_str = "rst_dev";
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_RST_ACK, NULL);
+		break;
+	default:
+		cmd_str = "unknown";
+		module_fwupd_send_status(state, HL_UPD_STS_VALID_ERR, NULL);
+		err = 1;
+		break;
+	}
+
+	if (!err)
+		module_fwupd_state_shift(state);
+
+	if (state->cmd.cmd != HL_UPD_CLR_STS)
+		LOG_INF("FW UPD %s  received", cmd_str);
+
+	if (err_str)
+		LOG_ERR("%s", err_str);
+
+	state->run_mode = NOT_FOUND;
+
+	watchdog_yield();
+}
+
+void module_fwupd_run(void)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = k_mutex_lock(&fwupd_mutex, K_MSEC(FWUPD_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_MS));
+	if (err == -1) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: mutex lock timeout\n", __func__);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	__module_fwupd_run(&state);
+
+	k_mutex_unlock(&fwupd_mutex);
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_preserve_boot_info(void)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	preboot_finalize_fw_info();
+	preboot_finalize_nic_en();
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_finalize();
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: failed in hl_boot_info_finalize, err: %d", __func__, err);
+}
+
+static void module_fwupd_run_noret_reset(void)
+{
+	module_fwupd_preserve_boot_info();
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_WFE);
+
+	arch_irq_lock();
+	hl_dev_hard_reset();	/* should not return */
+	CODE_UNREACHABLE;
+}
+
+void __module_fwupd_run_noret(struct fwupd_state *state, k_tid_t thread_id)
+{
+	/* terminate all other thread cmd modules */
+	switch (state->wfc_cmd.cmd) {
+	case HL_UPD_RST_DEV:
+		cmd_module_shutdown(thread_id, SHUTDOWN_MODE_SET_RESET_TIMEOUT);
+		preboot_if_shutdown();
+		module_fwupd_run_noret_reset();
+		break;
+	default:
+		arch_irq_lock();
+		fit_load_run();
+	}
+}
+
+void module_fwupd_run_noret(k_tid_t thread_id)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = k_mutex_lock(&fwupd_mutex, K_MSEC(FWUPD_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_MS));
+	if (err == -1) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: mutex lock timeout\n", __func__);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	__module_fwupd_run_noret(&state, thread_id);
+
+	k_mutex_unlock(&fwupd_mutex);
+}
+
+__weak void module_fwupd_dev_spec_shutdown(void)
+{
+	module_fwupd_internal_reset_state(&state);
+}
+
+void module_fwupd_shutdown(void)
+{
+	state.thread_can_run = false;
+	k_thread_join(fwupd_tid, FWUPD_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC);
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_fwupd_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return fwupd_tid;
+}
+
+#ifdef CONFIG_PREBOOT_MODULE_FWUPD_OOB
+
+#define FWUPD_ELBI_FIT_OFFSET_OOB	SZ_8K
+
+static void module_oob_fwupd_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3);
+static void module_oob_fwupd_run(void);
+static void module_oob_fwupd_run_noret(k_tid_t thread_id);
+
+#define MODULE_OOB_FWUPD_STACKSIZE	(4096 - CONFIG_HL_STACK_OVERFLOW_REDUCE_SIZE)
+static k_tid_t oob_fwupd_tid;
+static struct wdt_thread_desc *oob_thread_desc;
+static struct k_thread module_oob_fwupd_thread;
+static K_THREAD_STACK_DEFINE(module_oob_fwupd_stack, MODULE_OOB_FWUPD_STACKSIZE);
+static struct fwupd_state oob_state;
+
+static u32 oob_fwupd_cmd_readl(void)
+{
+	return oob_state.cmd_reg;
+}
+
+static void oob_fwupd_sts_writel(u32 val)
+{
+	oob_state.sts_reg = val;
+}
+
+bool oob_fwupd_owned(u32 offset)
+{
+	return offset == global_conf.upd_cmd_map || offset == global_conf.upd_sts_map;
+}
+
+/* This function is for reading status by other modules */
+u32 oob_fwupd_readl(u32 offset)
+{
+	return oob_state.sts_reg;
+}
+
+/* This function is for writing cmd by other modules */
+void oob_fwupd_writel(u32 offset, u32 val)
+{
+	oob_state.cmd_reg = val;
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_oob_fwupd_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	oob_state.cmd_readl = oob_fwupd_cmd_readl;
+	oob_state.sts_writel = oob_fwupd_sts_writel;
+	oob_state.use_security = false;
+	oob_state.synced_no_return = true;
+	oob_state.thread_can_run = true;
+	oob_state.elbi_offset = FWUPD_ELBI_FIT_OFFSET_OOB;
+	module_fwupd_send_status(&oob_state, HL_UPD_STS_RST_OK, NULL);
+
+	oob_fwupd_tid = k_thread_create(&module_oob_fwupd_thread, module_oob_fwupd_stack,
+					MODULE_OOB_FWUPD_STACKSIZE, module_oob_fwupd_check, NULL,
+					NULL, NULL, CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY, K_INHERIT_PERMS, K_NO_WAIT);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(&module_oob_fwupd_thread, thread_name);
+
+	return oob_fwupd_tid;
+}
+
+static void module_oob_fwupd_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3)
+{
+	while (oob_state.thread_can_run) {
+		if (wdt_is_initialized() &&
+			watchdog_watch_threads() &&
+			oob_thread_desc == NULL)
+			oob_thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+		__module_fwupd_check(&oob_state, oob_fwupd_tid, module_oob_fwupd_run,
+				     module_oob_fwupd_run_noret);
+
+		if (oob_thread_desc)
+			wdt_thread_keep_alive(oob_thread_desc);
+
+		 watchdog_yield();
+	}
+
+	module_oob_fwupd_dev_spec_shutdown();
+	if (oob_thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(oob_thread_desc->thread_id);
+		oob_thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+
+	preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(false);
+}
+
+void module_oob_fwupd_run(void)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = k_mutex_lock(&fwupd_mutex, K_MSEC(FWUPD_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_MS));
+	if (err == -1) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: mutex lock timeout\n", __func__);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	__module_fwupd_run(&oob_state);
+
+	k_mutex_unlock(&fwupd_mutex);
+}
+
+void module_oob_fwupd_run_noret(k_tid_t thread_id)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = k_mutex_lock(&fwupd_mutex, K_MSEC(FWUPD_MUTEX_TIMEOUT_MS));
+	if (err == -1) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: mutex lock timeout\n", __func__);
+		return;
+	}
+
+	__module_fwupd_run_noret(&oob_state, thread_id);
+
+	k_mutex_unlock(&fwupd_mutex);
+}
+
+__weak void module_oob_fwupd_dev_spec_shutdown(void)
+{
+	module_fwupd_internal_reset_state(&oob_state);
+}
+
+void module_oob_fwupd_shutdown(void)
+{
+	module_oob_fwupd_dev_spec_shutdown();
+	preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(false);
+	oob_state.thread_can_run = false;
+	k_thread_join(oob_fwupd_tid, FWUPD_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC);
+}
+
+bool module_oob_fwupd_is_allowed_access_range(uint32_t offset, uint32_t size)
+{
+	uintptr_t req_limit_addr, sect_limit_addr;
+
+	req_limit_addr = offset + size;
+	sect_limit_addr = (uintptr_t)oob_state.map_offset + oob_state.map_size;
+
+	if (offset >= (uintptr_t)oob_state.map_offset && req_limit_addr <= sect_limit_addr)
+		return true;
+
+	return false;
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_oob_fwupd_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return oob_fwupd_tid;
+}
+
+#endif /* CONFIG_PREBOOT_MODULE_FWUPD_OOB */

+#include <stdint.h>

+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <hl_fw_if.h> /* requires preboot.h for __packed */
+#include <boot_if.h>

+#include <util.h>
+#include <security.h>
+#include <crc.h>

+#include <eeprom.h>
+#include "oob_if.h"

+#include "cmd_module.h"
+#include "fit.h"
+#include "security.h"
+#include <watchdog_timer.h>
