+#define BIT_ULL(nr)	(1ULL << (nr))
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(module_lkd);
+
+static void module_lkd_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3);
+static void module_lkd_run(void);
+static void module_lkd_run_noret(void);
+
+struct lkd_status {
+	struct comms_command cmd;
+	struct comms_command wfc_cmd;
+	struct comms_command do_cmd;
+	enum cmd_check_res exec_res;
+	unsigned int next_size;
+	bool is_elbi_desc;
+	bool is_load_fit;
+	u32 fit_load_type;
+	struct lkd_fw_ascii_msg msg[LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX];
+	unsigned int lkd_ascii_msg_top;
+	bool thread_can_run;
+	uintptr_t mem_base;
+	int mem_size;
+};
+
+static struct lkd_status sts;
+
+#define MODULE_LKD_STACKSIZE		(5120 - CONFIG_HL_STACK_OVERFLOW_REDUCE_SIZE)
+#define LKD_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC	K_MSEC(1000)
+#define LKD_MAX_VALIDATION_TIME_MS	(5 * 60 * 1000)
+
+static k_tid_t thread_id;
+static struct wdt_thread_desc *thread_desc;
+static struct k_thread module_lkd_thread;
+static K_THREAD_STACK_DEFINE(module_lkd_stack, MODULE_LKD_STACKSIZE);
+
+int module_lkd_register_ascii_msg(enum lkd_fw_ascii_msg_lvls lvl, const char *msg)
+{
+	if (lvl < LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_ERR || lvl > LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_DBG) {
+		LOG_ERR("LKD: Invalid LKD message log level: %d", lvl);
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	if (sts.lkd_ascii_msg_top >= LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX) {
+		LOG_ERR("LKD: Messages stack is full");
+		return 2;
+	}
+
+	sts.msg[sts.lkd_ascii_msg_top].msg_lvl = lvl;
+	strncpy(sts.msg[sts.lkd_ascii_msg_top].msg, msg, LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX_LEN);
+	sts.msg[sts.lkd_ascii_msg_top].valid = 1;
+	sts.lkd_ascii_msg_top++;
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_clr_msgs(void)
+{
+	/* clear the registered messages */
+	memset(sts.msg, 0, sizeof(sts.msg));
+	sts.lkd_ascii_msg_top = 0;
+}
+
+__weak void lkd_dev_specific_enable(void)
+{
+	cpu_boot_dev_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_LD_COM_EN);
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_WAITING_FOR_BOOT_FIT);
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_enable(void)
+{
+	lkd_dev_specific_enable();
+}
+
+__weak void lkd_dev_specific_setup(void) {}
+
+k_tid_t module_lkd_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	lkd_dev_specific_setup();
+	module_lkd_enable();
+	sts.thread_can_run = true;
+
+	thread_id = k_thread_create(&module_lkd_thread, module_lkd_stack, MODULE_LKD_STACKSIZE,
+				    module_lkd_check, NULL, NULL, NULL, CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY,
+				    K_INHERIT_PERMS, K_NO_WAIT);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(&module_lkd_thread, thread_name);
+
+	return thread_id;
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_send_status(enum comms_sts sts, enum comms_ram_types ram_type,
+				   unsigned int offset)
+{
+	struct comms_status status;
+	u32 off = offset >> COMMS_STATUS_OFFSET_ALIGN_SHIFT;
+
+	status.val = 0;
+	status.status = sts;
+	status.ram_type = ram_type;
+	status.offset = off & COMMS_STATUS_OFFSET_MASK;
+	cpu_comms_sts_set(&status);
+}
+
+static bool is_legacy_comms(u32 msg)
+{
+	return msg > 0 && msg < KMD_MSG_LAST;
+}
+
+static enum cmd_check_res module_lkd_check_legacy(void)
+{
+	LOG_WRN("legacy LKD not supported.");
+
+	/* clear command register */
+	cpu_boot_cmd_clr_before_wfe();
+	sts.cmd.val = 0;
+
+	return NOT_FOUND;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_msg_reset_cause(struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *mcomms)
+{
+	int err = 1;
+
+	switch (mcomms->reset_cause) {
+	case HL_RESET_CAUSE_HEARTBEAT:
+		err = oob_set_wd_err(HL_FW_RESET_CAUSE_HEARTBEAT);
+		break;
+	case HL_RESET_CAUSE_TDR:
+		err = oob_set_wd_err(HL_FW_RESET_CAUSE_TDR);
+		/* no break */
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_msg_fw_cfg_skip(struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *mcomms)
+{
+	LOG_ERR("fw_cfg_skip is not supported");
+	return -1;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_msg_eeprom_defaults(struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *mcomms)
+{
+	if (preboot_security_is_enabled()) {
+		LOG_ERR("EEPROM defaults are not supported due to security");
+		return -1;
+	}
+
+	preboot_set_eeprom_defaults_en(mcomms->eeprom_defaults_on_err);
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_msg_process(struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *mcomms)
+{
+	int err = 1;
+	char *msg = "UNKNOWN";
+
+	switch (mcomms->header.type) {
+	case HL_COMMS_RESET_CAUSE_TYPE:
+		err = module_lkd_msg_reset_cause(mcomms);
+		msg = "RST_CAUSE";
+		break;
+	case HL_COMMS_FW_CFG_SKIP_TYPE:
+		err = module_lkd_msg_fw_cfg_skip(mcomms);
+		msg = "FW_CFG_SKIP";
+		break;
+	case HL_COMMS_EEPROM_DEFAULTS_TYPE:
+		err = module_lkd_msg_eeprom_defaults(mcomms);
+		msg = "EEPROM_DEFAULTS";
+		break;
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+
+	LOG_INF("Got host message: %s", msg);
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_sram_sf_access(void *fit)
+{
+	int err;
+	const void *data = 0;
+
+	err = fit_image_get_loadable_data(fit, &data, sizeof(u32));
+	if (!err)
+		err = preboot_set_sram_sf_access(*(u32 *)data);
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static int lkd_get_image_addr(struct mem_desc *desc)
+{
+	int err = 0;
+
+	if (sts.is_elbi_desc) {
+		err = preboot_get_elbi_reg_base(&desc->base);
+		err |= preboot_get_elbi_reg_size(&desc->size);
+	} else {
+		desc->base = sts.mem_base;
+		desc->size = sts.mem_size;
+	}
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_run_exec(void)
+{
+	struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *mcomms;
+	struct mem_desc desc;
+	int err;
+
+	err = lkd_get_image_addr(&desc);
+	if (err)
+		return err;
+
+	if (!sts.is_load_fit) {
+		mcomms = (struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *)desc.base;
+
+		return module_lkd_msg_process(mcomms);
+	}
+
+	switch (sts.fit_load_type) {
+	case FIT_LOAD_TYPE_SRAM_SF_ACCESS:
+		err = module_lkd_sram_sf_access((void *)desc.base);
+		preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_close();
+		preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(false);
+		break;
+	case FIT_LOAD_TYPE_RUN:
+		fit_run_image();
+		break;
+	default:
+		err = 1;
+	}
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_msg_validate(uintptr_t offset)
+{
+	struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *mcomms = (struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *)offset;
+	int err = 1;
+
+	if (HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALIDATE_MAGIC(mcomms->header.magic)) {
+		sts.exec_res = FOUND_RETURN;
+		err = 0;
+	}
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static int module_lkd_validate(void)
+{
+	int err;
+	struct mem_desc desc;
+	struct fit_error fit_err = {};
+
+	wdt_start_time_limited_section(LKD_MAX_VALIDATION_TIME_MS);
+	preboot_set_sram_sf_access(false);
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_sram_close();
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_close();
+
+	err = lkd_get_image_addr(&desc);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("LKD: failed requesting memory");
+		goto module_lkd_validate_fin;
+	}
+
+	err = fit_get_load_type((void *)desc.base, &sts.fit_load_type, &fit_err);
+	sts.is_load_fit = !err;
+
+	if (!sts.is_load_fit) {
+		/* shared mem doesn't contain fit image. */
+		err = module_lkd_msg_validate(desc.base);
+		if (err)
+			sts.exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+
+		goto module_lkd_validate_fin;
+	}
+
+	fit_print_versions((void *)desc.base);
+
+	switch (sts.fit_load_type) {
+	case FIT_LOAD_TYPE_SRAM_SF_ACCESS:
+		sts.exec_res = FOUND_RETURN;
+		break;
+	case FIT_LOAD_TYPE_RUN:
+	case FIT_LOAD_TYPE_RUN_N_EXTEND:
+		/* validate if shared memory has fit image */
+		err = fit_verify((void *)desc.base, &fit_err);
+		if (!err)
+			err = fit_load_images((void *)desc.base, &fit_err);
+
+		fit_report_error(&fit_err);
+		if (!err)
+			sts.exec_res = get_fit_no_ret() ? FOUND_NO_RETURN : FOUND_RETURN;
+
+		break;
+	default:
+		LOG_ERR("LKD: Unknown FIT load type");
+		err = 1;
+	}
+
+module_lkd_validate_fin:
+	wdt_stop_time_limited_section();
+	return err;
+}
+
+__weak u64 lkd_dev_spec_get_img_addr(uintptr_t base)
+{
+	return base;
+}
+
+__weak u32 lkd_dev_spec_get_desc_offset(uintptr_t base)
+{
+	return (u32)base;
+}
+
+__weak void lkd_dev_spec_fill_binning_info(struct lkd_fw_binning_info *bin_info) {}
+
+__weak u32 lkd_dev_spec_set_rsvd_mem_size(void) { return 0; }
+__weak u8 lkd_dev_spec_get_module_type(void) { return MODULE_TYPE_MEZZ; }
+
+static int module_lkd_prep_desc(int img_size, u32 *offset)
+{
+	struct mem_desc img_desc;
+	struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *cdesc;
+	int err, size;
+	const char *preboot_ver, *fuse_data;
+	u8 *cdesc_data_off;
+
+	size = ROUND_UP(img_size, SZ_4K);
+
+	if (!sts.mem_base) {
+		sts.mem_base = (uintptr_t)sys_heap_aligned_alloc(&mem_heap_main, SZ_4K, size);
+		if (!sts.mem_base) {
+			LOG_ERR("%s: Failed in alloc", __func__);
+			return 1;
+		}
+	}
+
+	sts.mem_size = size;
+
+	err = !preboot_is_valid_address_range(sts.mem_base, size);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Invalid address range 0x%lx size %d\n", __func__, sts.mem_base, size);
+
+	cdesc = (struct lkd_fw_comms_msg *)sts.mem_base;
+	memset(cdesc, 0, sizeof(*cdesc));
+
+	lkd_dev_spec_regs(&cdesc->cpu_dyn_regs);
+	fuse_data = preboot_get_version(PREBOOT_EFUSE_VER);
+	memcpy(cdesc->fuse_ver, fuse_data, VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+	preboot_ver = preboot_get_version(PREBOOT_BTL_VER);
+	memcpy(cdesc->cur_fw_ver, preboot_ver, VERSION_MAX_LEN);
+
+	err = lkd_get_image_addr(&img_desc);
+	if (err)
+		return err;
+
+	cdesc->img_addr = lkd_dev_spec_get_img_addr(img_desc.base);
+
+	memset(&cdesc->binning_info, 0, sizeof(cdesc->binning_info));
+	lkd_dev_spec_fill_binning_info(&cdesc->binning_info);
+	cdesc->rsvd_mem_size_mb = lkd_dev_spec_set_rsvd_mem_size();
+	cdesc->module_type = lkd_dev_spec_get_module_type();
+
+	/* copy any LKD message accumulated till now */
+	memcpy(cdesc->ascii_msg, sts.msg, sizeof(cdesc->ascii_msg));
+	/* here we implement "fire and forget" approach w/o ensuring receival */
+	module_lkd_clr_msgs();
+
+	/* Header */
+	cdesc->header.magic = HL_COMMS_DESC_MAGIC;
+	cdesc->header.size = sizeof(*cdesc) - sizeof(cdesc->header);
+	cdesc->header.version = HL_COMMS_DESC_VER;
+	cdesc_data_off = (u8 *)cdesc + sizeof(cdesc->header);
+	cdesc->header.crc32 = crc32_ieee_update(0, cdesc_data_off, cdesc->header.size);
+
+	*offset = lkd_dev_spec_get_desc_offset(sts.mem_base);
+
+	preboot_security_dynamic_img_sram_open(sts.mem_base, sts.mem_size, ACCESS_RW);
+
+	if (sts.is_elbi_desc) {
+		preboot_security_dynamic_img_elbi_reg_open();
+		err = preboot_elbi_mem_access_en(true);
+	}
+
+	return err;
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_reset_state(void)
+{
+	sts.exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+	sts.next_size = 0;
+	sts.cmd.val = 0;
+	sts.wfc_cmd.val = 0;
+	sts.do_cmd.val = 0;
+	sts.is_elbi_desc = false;
+	sts.is_load_fit = false;
+	sts.fit_load_type = 0;
+
+	if (sts.mem_base) {
+		memset((void *)sts.mem_base, 0, sts.mem_size);
+		sys_heap_free(&mem_heap_main, (void *)sts.mem_base);
+		sts.mem_base = 0;
+	}
+
+	sts.mem_size = 0;
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_run_noop(void)
+{
+	u32 offset = 0;
+	int err;
+
+	/* depending on the current state machine, perform action */
+	switch (sts.do_cmd.cmd) {
+	case COMMS_NOOP:
+	case COMMS_CLR_STS:
+	case COMMS_GOTO_WFE:
+	case COMMS_SKIP_BMC:
+	case COMMS_LOW_PLL_OPP:
+		module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_NOOP, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		break;
+	case COMMS_RST_STATE:
+		module_lkd_reset_state();
+		fit_clr_state();
+		module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_OK, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		break;
+	case COMMS_EXEC:
+		err = module_lkd_run_exec();
+		if (err)
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ERR, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		else
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_OK, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+
+		break;
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC:
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI:
+		sts.is_elbi_desc = sts.do_cmd.cmd == COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI;
+		err = module_lkd_prep_desc(sts.next_size, &offset);
+		if (err)
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ERR, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		else
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_OK, COMMS_SRAM, offset);
+		break;
+	case COMMS_DATA_RDY:
+		err = module_lkd_validate();
+		if (err)
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_VALID_ERR, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		else
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_OK, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+
+		break;
+	default:	/* should never get here */
+		LOG_ERR("%s: default case - err", __func__);
+	}
+}
+
+/* NOOP is a sync command, so here we decide what execution type is needed */
+static enum cmd_check_res module_lkd_check_noop(void)
+{
+	enum cmd_check_res res = NOT_FOUND;
+
+	/* check the command we got before waiting for sync */
+	switch (sts.do_cmd.cmd) {
+	case COMMS_EXEC:
+		res = sts.exec_res;
+		break;
+	case COMMS_RST_DEV:
+		res = FOUND_NO_RETURN;
+		break;
+	case COMMS_GOTO_WFE:
+		if (!preboot_security_is_enabled()) {
+			res = FOUND_NO_RETURN;
+			break;
+		}
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC:
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI:
+	case COMMS_DATA_RDY:
+	case COMMS_NOOP:
+	case COMMS_CLR_STS:
+	case COMMS_RST_STATE:
+	case COMMS_SKIP_BMC:
+	case COMMS_LOW_PLL_OPP:
+	default:
+		res = FOUND_RETURN;
+		break;
+	}
+
+	return res;
+}
+
+static enum cmd_check_res __module_lkd_check(void)
+{
+	enum cmd_check_res res = NOT_FOUND;
+
+	sts.cmd.val = cpu_boot_cmd_get();
+	if (is_legacy_comms(sts.cmd.val))
+		return module_lkd_check_legacy();
+
+	if (sts.cmd.cmd == sts.wfc_cmd.cmd)
+		return NOT_FOUND;
+
+	switch (sts.cmd.cmd) {
+	case COMMS_NOOP:	/* NOOP is a special command */
+		res = module_lkd_check_noop();
+		break;
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC:
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI:
+		sts.exec_res = NOT_FOUND;
+		sts.next_size = sts.cmd.size;
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_CLR_STS:
+	case COMMS_RST_STATE:
+	case COMMS_DATA_RDY:
+	case COMMS_EXEC:
+	case COMMS_RST_DEV:
+	case COMMS_GOTO_WFE:
+	case COMMS_SKIP_BMC:
+	case COMMS_LOW_PLL_OPP:
+		res = FOUND_RETURN;
+		break;
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+
+	return res;
+}
+
+static void __module_lkd_handle(enum cmd_check_res res)
+{
+	switch (res) {
+	case NOT_FOUND:
+		break;
+	case FOUND_RETURN:
+		module_lkd_run();
+		break;
+	case FOUND_NO_RETURN:
+		/* terminate all other thread cmd modules */
+		if (sts.do_cmd.cmd == COMMS_EXEC)
+			cmd_module_shutdown(thread_id, SHUTDOWN_MODE_SET_LOAD_IMAGE_TIMEOUT);
+		else if (sts.do_cmd.cmd == COMMS_RST_DEV)
+			cmd_module_shutdown(thread_id, SHUTDOWN_MODE_SET_RESET_TIMEOUT);
+		else
+			cmd_module_shutdown(thread_id, SHUTDOWN_MODE_DISABLE_WATCHDOG);
+		module_lkd_run_noret();
+		break;
+	default:
+		break;
+	}
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_disable(void)
+{
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_PREBOOT);
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_check(void *p1, void *p2, void *p3)
+{
+	enum cmd_check_res res;
+
+	while (sts.thread_can_run) {
+		if (wdt_is_initialized() &&
+			watchdog_watch_threads() &&
+			thread_desc == NULL)
+			thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+		cmd_module_common_check();
+		res = __module_lkd_check();
+		__module_lkd_handle(res);
+
+		if (thread_desc)
+			wdt_thread_keep_alive(thread_desc);
+
+		watchdog_yield();
+	}
+
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+
+	module_lkd_reset_state();
+	module_lkd_disable();
+}
+
+void module_lkd_shutdown(void)
+{
+	sts.thread_can_run = false;
+	k_thread_join(thread_id, LKD_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC);
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_state_shift(void)
+{
+	/* do not shift if got CLR_STS several times */
+	if (sts.cmd.cmd == COMMS_CLR_STS && sts.wfc_cmd.cmd == COMMS_CLR_STS)
+		return;
+
+	/* CLR_STS should not propagate to do_cmd */
+	sts.do_cmd.cmd = sts.wfc_cmd.cmd == COMMS_CLR_STS ? COMMS_NOOP : sts.wfc_cmd.cmd;
+	/* save current command waiting for clear, until handled */
+	sts.wfc_cmd = sts.cmd;
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_run(void)
+{
+	const char *cmd_str = NULL;
+	const char *err_str = NULL;
+
+	switch (sts.cmd.cmd) {
+	case COMMS_NOOP:
+		cmd_str = "noop";
+		module_lkd_run_noop();
+		break;
+	case COMMS_CLR_STS:
+		cmd_str = "clr_sts";
+		/* Clear the status */
+		module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_NOOP, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		break;
+	case COMMS_RST_STATE:
+		cmd_str = "rst_state";
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "prep_desc";
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "prep_desc_elbi";
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_DATA_RDY:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "data_rdy";
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_RST_DEV:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "rst_dev";
+		module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ACK, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		/* We ACK the command, but handle only on CLR, so save cmd */
+		break;
+	case COMMS_GOTO_WFE:
+		cmd_str = "wfe";
+		if (preboot_security_is_enabled()) {
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ERR, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+			err_str = "WFE denied - security is enabled...";
+		} else {
+			module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ACK, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		}
+
+		break;
+	case COMMS_EXEC:
+		cmd_str = "exec";
+		module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ACK, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+		break;
+	case COMMS_SKIP_BMC:
+		cmd_str = "skip_bmc";
+		/* no break */
+	case COMMS_LOW_PLL_OPP:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "low_pll_opp";
+		/* no break */
+	default:
+		cmd_str = cmd_str ?: "unknown";
+		module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_ERR, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+	}
+
+	/* no problem to shift on error, as later we check the validity */
+	module_lkd_state_shift();
+
+	LOG_INF("Got host %s command", cmd_str);
+
+	if (err_str)
+		LOG_ERR("%s", err_str);
+}
+
+/* For devices that do not implement RST_DEV command, mimic WFE */
+__weak void hl_dev_hard_reset(void) { wfe(); }
+__weak void preboot_ack_clear_irq(void) {}
+
+static void module_lkd_run_noret_exec(void)
+{
+	/*
+	 * List of status bits we clear because we do not know if the FW we are
+	 * loading supports these functionalities
+	 */
+	u64 sts_msk = BIT_ULL(CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_HARD_RST_EN) |
+		      BIT_ULL(CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_LD_COM_EN);
+
+	/*
+	 * Some events (like RAZWI or PCI ERR) might appear during boot
+	 * due to async access from host side to locked range registers (PCI)
+	 * (i.e. during iatu initlization or reset flow, PCI access is
+	 * disabled). Therefore this ack is needed.
+	 */
+	preboot_ack_clear_irq();
+
+	preboot_security_cleanup_before_run();
+	preboot_if_shutdown();
+	cpu_boot_dev_sts_clrset(sts_msk, false);
+
+	arch_irq_lock();
+
+	fit_run_image();
+}
+
+__weak void preboot_finalize_nic_en(void) {}
+
+static void module_lkd_preserve_sp_info(void)
+{
+	int ret;
+
+	preboot_finalize_fw_info();
+	preboot_finalize_nic_en();
+
+	ret = hl_boot_info_finalize();
+	if (ret)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: failed in module_lkd_preserve_sp_info, ret=%d", __func__, ret);
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_run_noret_reset(void)
+{
+	module_lkd_preserve_sp_info();
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_WFE);
+	preboot_if_shutdown();
+
+	arch_irq_lock();
+	hl_dev_hard_reset();	/* should not return */
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_run_noret_wfe(void)
+{
+	module_lkd_preserve_sp_info();
+
+	/* Exiting WFE will probably be done by SW reset */
+	hl_set_reset_cause(HL_SW_ALL_RST);
+
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_WFE);
+	preboot_if_shutdown();
+
+	arch_irq_lock();
+	wfe();
+}
+
+static void module_lkd_run_noret(void)
+{
+	/* we are not returning - clear comms status */
+	module_lkd_send_status(COMMS_STS_NOOP, COMMS_SRAM, 0);
+
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+
+	/* depending on the current state machine, perform action */
+	switch (sts.do_cmd.cmd) {
+	case COMMS_EXEC:
+		module_lkd_run_noret_exec();
+		break;
+	case COMMS_RST_DEV:
+		module_lkd_run_noret_reset();
+		break;
+	case COMMS_GOTO_WFE:
+		module_lkd_run_noret_wfe();
+		break;
+	default:	/* should never get here */
+		LOG_ERR("%s: default case - err", __func__);
+	}
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_lkd_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return thread_id;
+}

b/lib/lzo/lzo.h

+#ifndef __LZO_H__
+#define __LZO_H__
+/*
+ *  LZO Public Kernel Interface
+ *  A mini subset of the LZO real-time data compression library
+ *
+ *  Copyright (C) 1996-2005 Markus F.X.J. Oberhumer <markus@oberhumer.com>
+ *
+ *  The full LZO package can be found at:
+ *  http://www.oberhumer.com/opensource/lzo/
+ *
+ *  Changed for kernel use by:
+ *  Nitin Gupta <nitingupta910@gmail.com>
+ *  Richard Purdie <rpurdie@openedhand.com>
+ */
+
+#define LZO1X_MEM_COMPRESS	(16384 * sizeof(unsigned char *))
+#define LZO1X_1_MEM_COMPRESS	LZO1X_MEM_COMPRESS
+
+#define lzo1x_worst_compress(x) ((x) + ((x) / 16) + 64 + 3)
+
+/* This requires 'workmem' of size LZO1X_1_MEM_COMPRESS */
+int lzo1x_1_compress(const unsigned char *src, size_t src_len,
+			unsigned char *dst, size_t *dst_len, void *wrkmem);
+
+/* safe decompression with overrun testing */
+int lzo1x_decompress_safe(const unsigned char *src, size_t src_len,
+			unsigned char *dst, size_t *dst_len);
+
+/* decompress lzop format */
+int lzop_decompress(const unsigned char *src, size_t src_len,
+		    unsigned char *dst, size_t *dst_len);
+
+/* check if the header is valid (based on magic numbers) */
+bool lzop_is_valid_header(const unsigned char *src);
+
+/*
+ * Return values (< 0 = Error)
+ */
+#define LZO_E_OK			0
+#define LZO_E_ERROR			(-1)
+#define LZO_E_OUT_OF_MEMORY		(-2)
+#define LZO_E_NOT_COMPRESSIBLE		(-3)
+#define LZO_E_INPUT_OVERRUN		(-4)
+#define LZO_E_OUTPUT_OVERRUN		(-5)
+#define LZO_E_LOOKBEHIND_OVERRUN	(-6)
+#define LZO_E_EOF_NOT_FOUND		(-7)
+#define LZO_E_INPUT_NOT_CONSUMED	(-8)
+#define LZO_E_NOT_YET_IMPLEMENTED	(-9)
+
+#endif
new file mode 100644 index 0000000..bd3c3f3

b/lib/lzo/lzo1x_decompress.c

@@ -0,0 +1,360 @@
+/*
+ *  LZO1X Decompressor from MiniLZO
+ *
+ *  Copyright (C) 1996-2005 Markus F.X.J. Oberhumer <markus@oberhumer.com>
+ *
+ *  The full LZO package can be found at:
+ *  http://www.oberhumer.com/opensource/lzo/
+ *
+ *  Changed for kernel use by:
+ *  Nitin Gupta <nitingupta910@gmail.com>
+ *  Richard Purdie <rpurdie@openedhand.com>
+ */
+
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <string.h>
+
+#include "lzo.h"
+#include "unaligned.h"
+#include "lzodefs.h"
+
+#define HAVE_IP(x, ip_end, ip) ((size_t)(ip_end - ip) < (x))
+#define HAVE_OP(x, op_end, op) ((size_t)(op_end - op) < (x))
+#define HAVE_LB(m_pos, out, op) (m_pos < out || m_pos >= op)
+
+#define COPY4(dst, src)	\
+		put_unaligned(get_unaligned((const u32 *)(src)), (u32 *)(dst))
+
+static const unsigned char lzop_magic[] = {
+	0x89, 0x4c, 0x5a, 0x4f, 0x00, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a
+};
+
+#define HEADER_HAS_FILTER	0x00000800L
+
+
+bool lzop_is_valid_header(const unsigned char *src)
+{
+	int i;
+	/* read magic: 9 first bytes */
+	for (i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(lzop_magic); i++) {
+		if (*src++ != lzop_magic[i])
+			return false;
+	}
+	return true;
+}
+
+static inline const unsigned char *parse_header(const unsigned char *src)
+{
+	u16 version;
+	int i;
+
+	if (!lzop_is_valid_header(src))
+		return NULL;
+
+	/* skip header */
+	src += 9;
+
+	/* get version (2bytes), skip library version (2),
+	 * 'need to be extracted' version (2) and
+	 * method (1) */
+	version = sys_be16_to_cpu(*(u16 *)src);
+	src += 7;
+	if (version >= 0x0940)
+		src++;
+	if (sys_be32_to_cpu(*(u32 *)src) & HEADER_HAS_FILTER)
+		src += 4; /* filter info */
+
+	/* skip flags, mode and mtime_low */
+	src += 12;
+	if (version >= 0x0940)
+		src += 4;	/* skip mtime_high */
+
+	i = *src++;
+	/* don't care about the file name, and skip checksum */
+	src += i + 4;
+
+	return src;
+}
+
+int lzop_decompress(const unsigned char *src, size_t src_len,
+		    unsigned char *dst, size_t *dst_len)
+{
+	unsigned char *start = dst;
+	const unsigned char *send = src + src_len;
+	u32 slen, dlen;
+	size_t tmp, remaining;
+	int r;
+
+	src = parse_header(src);
+	if (!src)
+		return LZO_E_ERROR;
+
+	remaining = *dst_len;
+	while (src < send) {
+		/* read uncompressed block size */
+		dlen = sys_be32_to_cpu(*(u32 *)src);
+		src += 4;
+
+		/* exit if last block */
+		if (dlen == 0) {
+			*dst_len = dst - start;
+			return LZO_E_OK;
+		}
+
+		/* read compressed block size, and skip block checksum info */
+		slen = sys_be32_to_cpu(*(u32 *)src);
+		src += 8;
+
+		if (slen <= 0 || slen > dlen)
+			return LZO_E_ERROR;
+
+		/* abort if buffer ran out of room */
+		if (dlen > remaining)
+			return LZO_E_OUTPUT_OVERRUN;
+
+		/* When the input data is not compressed at all,
+		 * lzo1x_decompress_safe will fail, so call memcpy()
+		 * instead */
+		if (dlen == slen) {
+			memcpy(dst, src, slen);
+		} else {
+			/* decompress */
+			tmp = dlen;
+			r = lzo1x_decompress_safe((u8 *)src, slen, dst, &tmp);
+
+			if (r != LZO_E_OK) {
+				*dst_len = dst - start;
+				return r;
+			}
+
+			if (dlen != tmp)
+				return LZO_E_ERROR;
+		}
+
+		src += slen;
+		dst += dlen;
+		remaining -= dlen;
+	}
+
+	return LZO_E_INPUT_OVERRUN;
+}
+
+int lzo1x_decompress_safe(const unsigned char *in, size_t in_len,
+			unsigned char *out, size_t *out_len)
+{
+	const unsigned char * const ip_end = in + in_len;
+	unsigned char * const op_end = out + *out_len;
+	const unsigned char *ip = in, *m_pos;
+	unsigned char *op = out;
+	size_t t;
+
+	*out_len = 0;
+
+	if (*ip > 17) {
+		t = *ip++ - 17;
+		if (t < 4)
+			goto match_next;
+		if (HAVE_OP(t, op_end, op))
+			goto output_overrun;
+		if (HAVE_IP(t + 1, ip_end, ip))
+			goto input_overrun;
+		do {
+			*op++ = *ip++;
+		} while (--t > 0);
+		goto first_literal_run;
+	}
+
+	while ((ip < ip_end)) {
+		t = *ip++;
+		if (t >= 16)
+			goto match;
+		if (t == 0) {
+			if (HAVE_IP(1, ip_end, ip))
+				goto input_overrun;
+			while (*ip == 0) {
+				t += 255;
+				ip++;
+				if (HAVE_IP(1, ip_end, ip))
+					goto input_overrun;
+			}
+			t += 15 + *ip++;
+		}
+		if (HAVE_OP(t + 3, op_end, op))
+			goto output_overrun;
+		if (HAVE_IP(t + 4, ip_end, ip))
+			goto input_overrun;
+
+		COPY4(op, ip);
+		op += 4;
+		ip += 4;
+		if (--t > 0) {
+			if (t >= 4) {
+				do {
+					COPY4(op, ip);
+					op += 4;
+					ip += 4;
+					t -= 4;
+				} while (t >= 4);
+				if (t > 0) {
+					do {
+						*op++ = *ip++;
+					} while (--t > 0);
+				}
+			} else {
+				do {
+					*op++ = *ip++;
+				} while (--t > 0);
+			}
+		}
+
+first_literal_run:
+		t = *ip++;
+		if (t >= 16)
+			goto match;
+		m_pos = op - (1 + M2_MAX_OFFSET);
+		m_pos -= t >> 2;
+		m_pos -= *ip++ << 2;
+
+		if (HAVE_LB(m_pos, out, op))
+			goto lookbehind_overrun;
+
+		if (HAVE_OP(3, op_end, op))
+			goto output_overrun;
+		*op++ = *m_pos++;
+		*op++ = *m_pos++;
+		*op++ = *m_pos;
+
+		goto match_done;
+
+		do {
+match:
+			if (t >= 64) {
+				m_pos = op - 1;
+				m_pos -= (t >> 2) & 7;
+				m_pos -= *ip++ << 3;
+				t = (t >> 5) - 1;
+				if (HAVE_LB(m_pos, out, op))
+					goto lookbehind_overrun;
+				if (HAVE_OP(t + 3 - 1, op_end, op))
+					goto output_overrun;
+				goto copy_match;
+			} else if (t >= 32) {
+				t &= 31;
+				if (t == 0) {
+					if (HAVE_IP(1, ip_end, ip))
+						goto input_overrun;
+					while (*ip == 0) {
+						t += 255;
+						ip++;
+						if (HAVE_IP(1, ip_end, ip))
+							goto input_overrun;
+					}
+					t += 31 + *ip++;
+				}
+				m_pos = op - 1;
+				m_pos -= sys_le16_to_cpu(*(u16 *)ip) >> 2;
+				ip += 2;
+			} else if (t >= 16) {
+				m_pos = op;
+				m_pos -= (t & 8) << 11;
+
+				t &= 7;
+				if (t == 0) {
+					if (HAVE_IP(1, ip_end, ip))
+						goto input_overrun;
+					while (*ip == 0) {
+						t += 255;
+						ip++;
+						if (HAVE_IP(1, ip_end, ip))
+							goto input_overrun;
+					}
+					t += 7 + *ip++;
+				}
+				m_pos -= sys_le16_to_cpu(*(u16 *)ip) >> 2;
+				ip += 2;
+				if (m_pos == op)
+					goto eof_found;
+				m_pos -= 0x4000;
+			} else {
+				m_pos = op - 1;
+				m_pos -= t >> 2;
+				m_pos -= *ip++ << 2;
+
+				if (HAVE_LB(m_pos, out, op))
+					goto lookbehind_overrun;
+				if (HAVE_OP(2, op_end, op))
+					goto output_overrun;
+
+				*op++ = *m_pos++;
+				*op++ = *m_pos;
+				goto match_done;
+			}
+
+			if (HAVE_LB(m_pos, out, op))
+				goto lookbehind_overrun;
+			if (HAVE_OP(t + 3 - 1, op_end, op))
+				goto output_overrun;
+
+			if (t >= 2 * 4 - (3 - 1) && (op - m_pos) >= 4) {
+				COPY4(op, m_pos);
+				op += 4;
+				m_pos += 4;
+				t -= 4 - (3 - 1);
+				do {
+					COPY4(op, m_pos);
+					op += 4;
+					m_pos += 4;
+					t -= 4;
+				} while (t >= 4);
+				if (t > 0)
+					do {
+						*op++ = *m_pos++;
+					} while (--t > 0);
+			} else {
+copy_match:
+				*op++ = *m_pos++;
+				*op++ = *m_pos++;
+				do {
+					*op++ = *m_pos++;
+				} while (--t > 0);
+			}
+match_done:
+			t = ip[-2] & 3;
+			if (t == 0)
+				break;
+match_next:
+			if (HAVE_OP(t, op_end, op))
+				goto output_overrun;
+			if (HAVE_IP(t + 1, ip_end, ip))
+				goto input_overrun;
+
+			*op++ = *ip++;
+			if (t > 1) {
+				*op++ = *ip++;
+				if (t > 2)
+					*op++ = *ip++;
+			}
+
+			t = *ip++;
+		} while (ip < ip_end);
+	}
+
+	*out_len = op - out;
+	return LZO_E_EOF_NOT_FOUND;
+
+eof_found:
+	*out_len = op - out;
+	return (ip == ip_end ? LZO_E_OK :
+		(ip < ip_end ? LZO_E_INPUT_NOT_CONSUMED : LZO_E_INPUT_OVERRUN));
+input_overrun:
+	*out_len = op - out;
+	return LZO_E_INPUT_OVERRUN;
+
+output_overrun:
+	*out_len = op - out;
+	return LZO_E_OUTPUT_OVERRUN;
+
+lookbehind_overrun:
+	*out_len = op - out;
+	return LZO_E_LOOKBEHIND_OVERRUN;
+}

+							<< (s1)) ^ (p)[0])
+#define DX3(p, s1, s2, s3)	((DX2((p)+1, s2, s3) << (s1)) ^ (p)[0])

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+ *
+ * Copyright (C) 2020 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ */
+
+#ifndef _GENERIC_UNALIGNED_H
+#define _GENERIC_UNALIGNED_H
+
+#include <types.h>
+#include <byteorder.h>
+#include "unaligned/generic.h"
+
+#ifndef __force
+#define __force
+#endif
+
+/*
+ * Select endianness
+ */
+#if __BYTE_ORDER == __LITTLE_ENDIAN
+#define get_unaligned	__get_unaligned_le
+#define put_unaligned	__put_unaligned_le
+#elif __BYTE_ORDER == __BIG_ENDIAN
+#define get_unaligned	__get_unaligned_be
+#define put_unaligned	__put_unaligned_be
+#else
+#error invalid endian
+#endif
+
+#endif

b/lib/lzo/unaligned/generic.h

@@ -0,0 +1,68 @@
+#ifndef _LINUX_UNALIGNED_GENERIC_H
+#define _LINUX_UNALIGNED_GENERIC_H
+
+/*
+ * Cause a link-time error if we try an unaligned access other than
+ * 1,2,4 or 8 bytes long
+ */
+extern void __bad_unaligned_access_size(void);
+
+#define __get_unaligned_le(ptr) ((__force __typeof__(*(ptr)))({			\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 1, *(ptr),			\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 2, sys_le16_to_cpu((ptr)),	\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 4, sys_le32_to_cpu((ptr)),	\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 8, sys_le64_to_cpu((ptr)),	\
+	__bad_unaligned_access_size()))));					\
+	}))
+
+#define __get_unaligned_be(ptr) ((__force __typeof__(*(ptr)))({			\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 1, *(ptr),			\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 2, sys_be16_to_cpu((ptr)),	\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 4, sys_be32_to_cpu((ptr)),	\
+	__builtin_choose_expr(sizeof(*(ptr)) == 8, sys_be64_to_cpu((ptr)),	\
+	__bad_unaligned_access_size()))));					\
+	}))
+
+#define __put_unaligned_be(val, ptr) ({					\
+	void *__gu_p = (ptr);						\
+	switch (sizeof(*(ptr))) {					\
+	case 1:								\
+		*(u8 *)__gu_p = (__force u8)(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	case 2:								\
+		*(u16 *)__gu_p = sys_cpu_to_be16(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	case 4:								\
+		*(u32 *)__gu_p = sys_cpu_to_be32(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	case 8:								\
+		*(u64 *)__gu_p = sys_cpu_to_be64(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	default:							\
+		__bad_unaligned_access_size();				\
+		break;							\
+	}								\
+	(void)0; })
+
+#define __put_unaligned_le(val, ptr) ({					\
+	void *__gu_p = (ptr);						\
+	switch (sizeof(*(ptr))) {					\
+	case 1:								\
+		*(u8 *)__gu_p = (__force u8)(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	case 2:								\
+		*(u16 *)__gu_p = sys_cpu_to_le16(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	case 4:								\
+		*(u32 *)__gu_p = sys_cpu_to_le32(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	case 8:								\
+		*(u64 *)__gu_p = sys_cpu_to_le64(val);			\
+		break;							\
+	default:							\
+		__bad_unaligned_access_size();				\
+		break;							\
+	}								\
+	(void)0; })
+
+#endif /* _LINUX_UNALIGNED_GENERIC_H */

b/lib/ras/pldm/pldm.c

@@ -0,0 +1,201 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright 2024 Habana Labs
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+#include <kernel.h>
+#include <memory.h>
+#include <common.h>
+#include <hl_fw_info.h>
+#include <fw_info.h>
+#include <pldm.h>
+#include <pldm_fu.h>
+#include <ras/pldm/pldm_pdr.h>
+#include <ras/pldm/pldm_effecter_db.h>
+#include <boot_if.h>
+#include <cmd_module.h>
+#include <shutdown.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <pldm_versions.h>
+#include "pldm_fu.h"
+#include <watchdog_timer.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(preboot_pldm);
+
+#define MODULE_PLDM_STACKSIZE (2048 - CONFIG_HL_STACK_OVERFLOW_REDUCE_SIZE)
+
+#define PLDM_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC 50
+
+static k_tid_t thread_id;
+static struct wdt_thread_desc *thread_desc;
+static struct k_thread module_pldm_thread;
+static bool thread_can_run = true;
+static K_THREAD_STACK_DEFINE(module_pldm_stack, MODULE_PLDM_STACKSIZE);
+
+static PLDM_DEFINE(pldm);
+
+__weak bool pldm_is_reset_requested(void)
+{
+	return false;
+}
+
+static void module_pldm_run_noret(void)
+{
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+
+	preboot_if_shutdown();
+	arch_irq_lock();
+	cpu_boot_sts_set(CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_WFE);
+	hl_dev_hard_reset();	/* should not return */
+	CODE_UNREACHABLE;
+}
+
+static void module_pldm_thread_fn(void *arg1, void *arg2, void *arg3)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	while (thread_can_run) {
+		LOG_DBG("%s: recv...", __func__);
+
+		if (wdt_is_initialized() &&
+			watchdog_watch_threads() &&
+			thread_desc == NULL)
+			thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+		if (thread_desc)
+			wdt_thread_keep_alive(thread_desc);
+
+		err = pldm_recv(&pldm, K_MSEC(CONFIG_LIBPLDM_RECV_TIMEOUT_MS));
+		if (err && err != -EAGAIN && err != -ETIMEDOUT)
+			LOG_WRN("%s: pldm_recv failed: %d", __func__, err);
+
+		if (pldm_is_reset_requested()) {
+			cmd_module_shutdown(thread_id, SHUTDOWN_MODE_SET_RESET_TIMEOUT);
+			module_pldm_run_noret();
+		}
+
+		pldm_tick(&pldm);
+	}
+
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+}
+
+static int module_pldm_init_msg_buffer(struct pldm_init_data *data)
+{
+	data->msg_buf = sys_heap_alloc(&mem_heap_main, sizeof(struct pldm_transport_msg));
+	if (!data->msg_buf) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: failed to allocate msg buffer", __func__);
+		return -1;
+	}
+
+	LOG_INF("%s: pldm msg offload: addr 0x%08x len %u",
+		__func__, (uint32_t)data->msg_buf, sizeof(struct pldm_transport_msg));
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static const struct pldm_fu_platform_api fu_api = {
+	.verify		= fu_verify,
+	.apply		= fu_apply,
+	.apply_post	= fu_apply_post,
+	.cancel		= fu_cancel,
+	.reset		= fu_reset,
+};
+
+k_tid_t module_pldm_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	struct pldm_init_data data = {
+		.dev = DEVICE_DT_GET(DT_INST(0, hl_mctp_smbus)),
+		.msg_buf = NULL,
+	};
+	const struct pldm_pmc_init_data pmc_data = {
+		.records = pldm_pdr_records,
+		.num_records = pldm_pdr_records_length,
+		.name_records = pldm_pdr_name_records,
+		.num_name_records = pldm_pdr_name_records_length,
+		.numeric_effecters = numeric_effecter_db,
+		.num_numeric_effecters = numeric_effecter_db_length,
+		.state_effecters = state_effecter_db,
+		.num_state_effecters = state_effecter_db_length,
+	};
+	const struct pldm_fu_init_data fu_data = {
+		.api = fu_api,
+		.image_buffer = (void *) HL_PLDM_FU_IMAGE_BASE,
+		.image_buffer_size = HL_PLDM_FU_IMAGE_SIZE,
+	};
+
+	if (module_pldm_init_msg_buffer(&data)) {
+		thread_can_run = false;
+		return 0;
+	}
+
+	pldm_init_ex(&pldm, &data);
+	pldm_pmc_init_ex(&pldm, &pmc_data);
+	pldm_fu_init(&pldm, &fu_data);
+
+	thread_id = k_thread_create(
+		&module_pldm_thread,
+		module_pldm_stack,
+		MODULE_PLDM_STACKSIZE,
+		module_pldm_thread_fn,
+		NULL,
+		NULL,
+		NULL,
+		CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY,
+		K_INHERIT_PERMS,
+		K_NO_WAIT);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(&module_pldm_thread, thread_name);
+
+	return thread_id;
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_pldm_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return thread_id;
+}
+
+void module_pldm_shutdown(void)
+{
+	k_timeout_t timeout = K_MSEC(CONFIG_LIBPLDM_RECV_TIMEOUT_MS
+				   + CONFIG_LIBPLDM_PROCESS_TIMEOUT_MS);
+
+	thread_can_run = false;
+	k_thread_join(thread_id, timeout);
+}
+
+__weak int hl_dev_get_pldm_versions(struct hl_component_versions **vers)
+{
+	LOG_ERR("PLDM versions not available for device");
+
+	return -1;
+}
+
+int pldm_send_versions(void)
+{
+	struct hl_component_versions *vers;
+	int err;
+
+	err = hl_dev_get_pldm_versions(&vers);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to get PLDM versions");
+		return err;
+	}
+
+	err = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_PLDM_VERSIONS, vers, vers->struct_size);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Failed to set boot info (%d)\n", __func__, err);
+		return err;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}

b/lib/ras/pldm/pldm_fu.c

@@ -0,0 +1,161 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright 2024 Habana Labs
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+#include <zephyr.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <app_wrq.h>
+#include <pldm.h>
+#include <pldm_fu.h>
+#include <ras/pldm/pldm_pdr.h>
+#include <ras/pldm/pldm_effecter_db.h>
+
+#include <fit.h>
+#include <fit_load.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(preboot_lib_pldm_fu);
+
+struct fu_work {
+	struct k_work work;
+	void *base_addr;
+};
+
+static void fu_verify_work(struct k_work *work);
+static void fu_apply_work(struct k_work *work);
+
+static struct fu_work fu_verify_data = { .work = Z_WORK_INITIALIZER(fu_verify_work), };
+static struct fu_work fu_apply_data = { .work = Z_WORK_INITIALIZER(fu_apply_work), };
+
+static void fu_verify_work(struct k_work *work)
+{
+	struct fit_error fit_err = {};
+	int err;
+
+	LOG_INF("PLDM verifying %p", fu_verify_data.base_addr);
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(10);
+	err = fit_verify(fu_verify_data.base_addr, &fit_err);
+	if (!err)
+		LOG_INF("Verified %p", fu_verify_data.base_addr);
+
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(90);
+	fit_report_error(&fit_err);
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(100);
+
+	switch (fit_err.load) {
+	case FIT_LOAD_SUCCESS:
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_VERIFY_RESULT_COMPLETE_WITHOUT_ERROR);
+		break;
+	case FIT_LOAD_SEC_FAILED:
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_VERIFY_RESULT_COMPLETE_IMAGE_FAILED_SECURITY_CHECKS);
+		break;
+	case FIT_LOAD_CORRUPTED:
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_VERIFY_RESULT_COMPLETE_WITH_VERIFICATION_ERROR);
+		break;
+	default:
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_VERIFY_RESULT_GENERIC_ERROR);
+		break;
+	}
+}
+
+static void fu_apply_work(struct k_work *work)
+{
+	int err;
+	bool is_run;
+	struct fit_error fit_err = {};
+
+	LOG_INF("PLDM applying %p", fu_apply_data.base_addr);
+
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(20);
+
+	err = fit_load_type(fu_apply_data.base_addr, false, &is_run, &fit_err);
+
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(90);
+	fit_report_error(&fit_err);
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(100);
+	if (!err) {
+		LOG_INF("Applied %p, %s", fu_apply_data.base_addr,
+			is_run ? "running..." : "nothing to run");
+
+		/* In case FIT is runnable, it needs to send apply complete message once loaded */
+		if (is_run)
+			pldm_fu_request_reset();
+		else
+			pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_APPLY_RESULT_COMPLETE_WITHOUT_ERROR);
+	} else if (fit_err.load == FIT_LOAD_BAD_LOAD_ADDR) {
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_APPLY_RESULT_COMPLETE_WITH_FAILURE_DUE_TO_MEM_WRITE);
+	} else {
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_APPLY_RESULT_GENERIC_ERROR);
+	}
+}
+
+int fu_verify(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component, void *base_addr)
+{
+	int ret;
+
+	LOG_INF("PLDM will verify %p", base_addr);
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(1);
+
+	fu_verify_data.base_addr = base_addr;
+	ret = k_work_submit_to_queue(&app_wrq, &fu_verify_data.work);
+	if (ret < 0) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to schedule pldm fu work %d", ret);
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_APPLY_RESULT_GENERIC_ERROR);
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+
+}
+
+int fu_apply(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component, void *base_addr)
+{
+	int ret;
+
+	pldm_fu_set_progress(10);
+
+	fu_apply_data.base_addr = base_addr;
+	ret = k_work_submit_to_queue(&app_wrq, &fu_apply_data.work);
+	if (ret < 0) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to schedule pldm fu work %d", ret);
+		pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_APPLY_RESULT_GENERIC_ERROR);
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+int fu_reset(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component, void *base_addr)
+{
+	/* From preboot, the only reset is to jump to the fw-loader agent */
+	fit_load_run();
+
+	CODE_UNREACHABLE;
+	return -1;
+}
+
+int fu_apply_post(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component)
+{
+	/* If this function is called, it is because the fw-loader agent was running
+	 * and while in the APPLY state it issues a soft reset to preboot.
+	 *
+	 * With the current FU design, this transition through preboot only occurs
+	 * as a validation step; we are now running the newly installed preboot, and
+	 * it is expected that the UA will next load another fw-loader agent image.
+	 * This time to update SPI flash marking this new image as the current
+	 * image.
+	 *
+	 * As such, no real work to do here; just mark APPLY as complete; this will
+	 * result in the UA sending the next fw-loader agent component.
+	 */
+	pldm_fu_set_complete(PLDM_FU_APPLY_RESULT_COMPLETE_WITHOUT_ERROR);
+	return 0;
+}
+
+void fu_cancel(pldm_fu_fsm_state_t state,
+	       struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component)
+{
+	k_work_cancel(&fu_verify_data.work);
+	k_work_cancel(&fu_apply_data.work);
+}

b/lib/ras/pldm/pldm_fu.h

@@ -0,0 +1,17 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ *
+ * Copyright (c) 2024 Habana Labs
+ */
+
+#ifndef PREBOOT_SUBSYS_PLDM_FU_H_
+#define PREBOOT_SUBSYS_PLDM_FU_H_
+
+#include <pldm.h>
+
+int fu_verify(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component, void *base_addr);
+int fu_apply(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component, void *base_addr);
+int fu_apply_post(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component);
+int fu_reset(struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component, void *base_addr);
+void fu_cancel(pldm_fu_fsm_state_t state, struct pldm_fu_update_component_req *component);
+
+#endif /* PREBOOT_SUBSYS_PLDM_FU_H_ */

b/lib/ras/pldm/sensord.c

@@ -0,0 +1,86 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (c) 2024 Habana Labs
+ */
+
+#include <kernel.h>
+#include <ras/pldm/sensord_db.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <watchdog_timer.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(preboot_sensord);
+
+#define MODULE_SENSORD_STACKSIZE (2048 - CONFIG_HL_STACK_OVERFLOW_REDUCE_SIZE)
+
+#define SENSORD_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC K_MSEC(1000)
+
+static k_tid_t thread_id;
+static struct wdt_thread_desc *thread_desc;
+static struct k_thread module_sensord_thread;
+static bool thread_can_run = true;
+static K_THREAD_STACK_DEFINE(module_sensord_stack, MODULE_SENSORD_STACKSIZE);
+
+static void module_sensord_thread_fn(void *arg1, void *arg2, void *arg3)
+{
+	while (thread_can_run) {
+		if (wdt_is_initialized() &&
+			watchdog_watch_threads() &&
+			thread_desc == NULL)
+			thread_desc = wdt_thread_created(k_current_get());
+
+		/* FIXME: SW-198643: remove this once sensord supports per-sensor sampling rates*/
+		hl_temp_sensors_update();
+
+		sensord_update();
+		k_sleep(K_MSEC(1));
+
+		if (thread_desc)
+			wdt_thread_keep_alive(thread_desc);
+	}
+
+	if (thread_desc) {
+		wdt_thread_finished(thread_desc->thread_id);
+		thread_desc = NULL;
+	}
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_sensord_setup(const char *thread_name)
+{
+	int err;
+
+	err = sensord_init(sensord_db, sensord_db_length);
+	if (err) {
+		LOG_ERR("%s: sensord_init failed: err %d", __func__, err);
+		return 0;
+	}
+
+	thread_id = k_thread_create(
+		&module_sensord_thread,
+		module_sensord_stack,
+		MODULE_SENSORD_STACKSIZE,
+		module_sensord_thread_fn,
+		NULL,
+		NULL,
+		NULL,
+		CONFIG_MAIN_THREAD_PRIORITY,
+		K_INHERIT_PERMS,
+		K_NO_WAIT);
+
+	k_thread_name_set(&module_sensord_thread, thread_name);
+
+	return thread_id;
+}
+
+k_tid_t module_sensord_tid_get(void)
+{
+	return thread_id;
+}
+
+void module_sensord_shutdown(void)
+{
+	thread_can_run = false;
+	k_thread_join(thread_id, SENSORD_WAIT_THREAD_STOP_MSEC);
+	preboot_hl_pvt_hd_cores_sensors_stop();
+}

b/lib/security/security.c

@@ -0,0 +1,249 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright 2023 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+#include <preboot.h>
+#include <memory.h>
+#include <security.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#include <efuse.h>
+#include "sha.h"
+#include <sha512.h>
+#include <mbedtls/sha512.h>
+#include <preboot/fit.h>
+#include <hl_fw_if.h>
+#include <preboot/fit_rsa.h>
+#include <preboot/fit.h>
+#include <boot_if.h>
+#include <fw_info.h>
+#include <hl_sec_if.h>
+#include <hl_fit_if.h>
+#include <hl_boot_info.h>
+#include <hl_boot_desc.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(common_security);
+static struct hl_fit_sec_desc fit_sec;
+static u8 signature[FIT_SIGNATURE_SIZE];
+
+__weak int preboot_security_get_minimal_svn(void)
+{
+	return 0;
+}
+
+bool preboot_security_svn_is_valid(int sec_ver)
+{
+	int min_svn = preboot_security_get_minimal_svn();
+
+	if (min_svn < 0)
+		return false;
+
+	fw_info.sec_min_svn = min_svn;
+
+	if (sec_ver < min_svn || sec_ver > SVN_MAX_VERSIONS) {
+		LOG_ERR("Illegal security version: %d", sec_ver);
+		return false;
+	}
+
+	return true;
+}
+
+__weak void hl_tpm_pcr_extend(u8 *fit_hash)
+{
+	return;
+}
+
+__weak bool preboot_skip_img_sig_validation(void) { return false; }
+
+__weak void hl_boot_fit_hash_store(u8 *fit_hash) { }
+
+void preboot_extend_fit_tpm_pcr(const void *img, size_t img_size)
+{
+	u8 fit_hash[SHA384_SUM_LEN];
+
+	if (preboot_skip_img_sig_validation())
+		return;
+
+	/* hl fit files are padded so the size is 4 bytes aligned */
+	img_size = ROUND_UP4(img_size);
+
+	mbedtls_sha512_ret(img, img_size, fit_hash, 1);
+	hl_tpm_pcr_extend(fit_hash);
+	hl_boot_fit_hash_store(fit_hash);
+}
+
+int preboot_verify_img_sig(const void *img, size_t img_size, enum fit_sec_failure *res,
+				u32 *key_idx)
+{
+	const void *fit_sec_off;
+	const void *pub_key_fit;
+
+	if (preboot_skip_img_sig_validation())
+		return 0;
+
+	/* hl fit files are padded so the size is 4 bytes aligned */
+	img_size = ROUND_UP4(img_size);
+
+	if (!preboot_img_validation_is_enabled())
+		return 0;
+
+	/* security descriptor is at the footer of the image */
+	fit_sec_off = (const void *)((u8 *)img + img_size);
+
+	/* check that the calculated memory range are legal */
+	if (!preboot_sram_is_valid_address_range((uintptr_t)fit_sec_off,
+						 sizeof(fit_sec))) {
+		LOG_ERR("Invalid address range");
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	memcpy(&fit_sec, fit_sec_off, FIT_SEC_DESC_SIZE);
+
+	if (fit_sec.magic_number != FIT_SEC_MAGIC) {
+		LOG_ERR("Bad magic, security descriptor not found");
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	if ((fit_sec.desc_version_maj != FIT_SEC_DESC_VER_MAJOR) ||
+	    (fit_sec.desc_version_min != FIT_SEC_DESC_VER_MINOR)) {
+		LOG_ERR("security descriptor version mismatch");
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	if (fit_sec.desc_size < FIT_SEC_DESC_SIZE) {
+		LOG_ERR("security descriptor size too small");
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	if (fit_sec.img_size != img_size) {
+		LOG_ERR("image size mismatch with security descriptor");
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	if (!preboot_security_svn_is_valid(fit_sec.sec_version)) {
+		LOG_ERR("security descriptor svn not valid");
+		*res = FIT_SEC_SVN_INVALID;
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	fw_info.sec_curr_svn = fit_sec.sec_version;
+
+	if (preboot_security_key_is_revoked(fit_sec.key_num)) {
+		LOG_INF("Key revoked: %d", fit_sec.key_num);
+		*res = FIT_SEC_KEY_REVOKED;
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	fw_info.sec_curr_key = fit_sec.key_num;
+	pub_key_fit = &fit_sec.pub_key;
+
+	if (!preboot_security_key_is_valid(fit_sec.key_num, pub_key_fit,
+					   FIT_PUB_KEY_SIZE, NULL, 0)) {
+		LOG_ERR("Key validation failed");
+		*res = FIT_SEC_SIGN_FAILED;
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	/*
+	 * signature is calculated on both the fit image and the security
+	 * descriptor with the signature field of the descriptor zeroized.
+	 */
+	memcpy(signature, fit_sec.signature, FIT_SIGNATURE_SIZE);
+	memset(fit_sec.signature, 0, FIT_SIGNATURE_SIZE);
+
+	if (fit_verify_img_sig(img, img_size, &fit_sec, FIT_SEC_DESC_SIZE,
+			       pub_key_fit, signature, FIT_SIGNATURE_SIZE)) {
+		LOG_ERR("image signature verification failed");
+		*res = FIT_SEC_SIGN_FAILED;
+		return 1;
+	}
+
+	*key_idx = fit_sec.key_num;
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+int hl_sec_calculate_svn(const u32 *svn_bits)
+{
+	int i, svn = 0;
+
+	for (i = 0; i < SVN_EFUSE_SIZE/sizeof(u32); i++) {
+		/* No bits are set, stop counting */
+		if (!svn_bits[i])
+			break;
+		/* All bits are set, move to next iteration, bits are counted
+		 * by the iterator at the end. Move to the next iteration.
+		 */
+		if (!~svn_bits[i])
+			continue;
+
+		/* Some bits are set, count them by __builtin_ctz() the bitwise
+		 * NOT value of the SVN since __builtin_ctz() count unset bits.
+		 */
+		svn += __builtin_ctz(~svn_bits[i]);
+		break;
+	}
+	/* Add bits counted by iterator in case that all the bits are set */
+	svn += i * 32;
+
+	/*
+	 * SVN was computed by counting no of set bits, however due to bug in arcboot rom an
+	 * addtional bit is programmed in efuse hence need to subract the svn by 1 bit.
+	 * ex: efuse val = 0xFF when SVN = 7, efuse val = 0x7F when SVN = 6
+	 */
+	if (svn)
+		svn--;
+
+	LOG_INF("SVN on eFuse: %d", svn);
+
+	return svn;
+}
+
+bool hl_extract_hash_from_key(const void *pub_key, u8 *value)
+{
+	struct rsa_key_prop prop;
+	u8 key_props[PUB_KEY_SZ];
+	const char *key_name = HL_SEC_KEY_NAME;
+
+	/*
+	 * ROM and eFUSE stores hash of public key properties. The public
+	 * key received as part of fit is the complete public key dtb of
+	 * 3144-bytes. Extract key properties and compute hash on 784 bytes
+	 * of key properties to compare with stored ROM/eFUSE hash.
+	 */
+	if (fit_get_key_prop(pub_key, key_name, &prop)) {
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to get key properties");
+		return false;
+	}
+
+	memset(key_props, 0, sizeof(key_props));
+	memcpy(key_props, prop.rr, KEY_PROP_RR_SZ);
+	memcpy((key_props + KEY_PROP_RR_SZ), prop.modulus, KEY_PROP_MOD_SZ);
+	memcpy((key_props + KEY_PROP_RR_SZ + KEY_PROP_MOD_SZ), prop.public_exponent,
+		KEY_PROP_EXP_SZ);
+	memcpy((key_props + KEY_PROP_RR_SZ + KEY_PROP_MOD_SZ + KEY_PROP_EXP_SZ),
+		&prop.n0inv, KEY_PROP_INV_SZ);
+	memcpy((key_props + KEY_PROP_RR_SZ + KEY_PROP_MOD_SZ + KEY_PROP_EXP_SZ +
+		KEY_PROP_INV_SZ), &prop.num_bits, KEY_PROP_NB_SZ);
+
+	sha_csum(key_props, PUB_KEY_SZ, value);
+
+	return true;
+}
+
+void preboot_security_boot_info_write_key_idx(u32 *key_idx)
+{
+	int ret = hl_boot_info_set(HL_BOOT_INFO_KEY_IDX, key_idx, sizeof(u32));
+
+	if (ret)
+		LOG_ERR("%s: Failed to set boot info (%d)\n", __func__, ret);
+}
+
+bool hl_is_security_enabled(void)
+{
+	return preboot_security_is_enabled();
+}

+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright 2023 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+#include <shell/shell_uart.h>
+#include <shell/shell.h>
+#include <cmd_shell.h>
+#include <security.h>
+#include <preboot.h>
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_LOG_DEFAULT_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(cmd_shell);
+
+static uint8_t *prompt_name = HL_SHELL_PROMPT_NAME;
+
+void preboot_shell_activate_cmds(void)
+{
+	const struct shell *shell = shell_backend_uart_get_ptr();
+	int err;
+
+	if (!shell) {
+		LOG_ERR("couldn't find shell backend");
+		return;
+	}
+
+	/* Shell commands are not supported in secured setups */
+	if (preboot_security_is_enabled())
+		return;
+
+	err = shell_set_auth_level(shell, SHELL_ROOT_AUTH_LEVEL);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("couldn't activate shell commands (err=%d)\n", err);
+}
+
+void preboot_shell_set_prompt_name(void)
+{
+	const struct shell *shell = shell_backend_uart_get_ptr();
+	int err;
+
+	if (!shell) {
+		LOG_ERR("couldn't find shell backend");
+		return;
+	}
+
+	hl_set_prompt_name(prompt_name);
+
+	err = shell_prompt_change(shell, prompt_name);
+	if (err)
+		LOG_ERR("Failed to set prompt name to %s (err=%d)", prompt_name, err);
+}
+
+static int dbg_ctrl(const struct shell *sh, size_t argc, char **argv)
+{
+	bool dbg_ena;
+
+	dbg_ena = preboot_get_dbg_prints_ena();
+	preboot_set_dbg_prints_ena(!dbg_ena);
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static int version_print(const struct shell *sh, size_t argc, char **argv)
+{
+	ARG_UNUSED(argc);
+	ARG_UNUSED(argv);
+
+	struct hl_component_versions *ver_comp = preboot_ver_get();
+	struct hl_module_data *module;
+	int mod;
+
+	if (!ver_comp) {
+		shell_print(sh, "invalid preboot version");
+		return -1;
+	}
+
+	module = (struct hl_module_data *)((char *)ver_comp + ver_comp->modules_offset);
+	shell_print(sh, "%s:", ver_comp->comp_name);
+	shell_print(sh, "\tcomponent: %s", ver_comp->component);
+	shell_print(sh, "\tfw_os: %s", ver_comp->fw_os);
+
+	for (mod = 0; mod < ver_comp->modules_counter; mod++) {
+		if (strcmp(module->name, "") && strcmp(module->version, ""))
+			shell_print(sh, "\t\t%s: %s", module->name, module->version);
+
+		module++;
+	}
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+SHELL_CMD_REGISTER(dbg, NULL,
+		   "Enable/Disable dbg prints",
+		   dbg_ctrl);
+
+SHELL_CMD_REGISTER(ver, NULL,
+		   "print version of running FW",
+		   version_print);

b/lib/tpm/tpm_auth.c

@@ -0,0 +1,159 @@
+// SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0
+/*
+ * Copyright (C) 2021 HabanaLabs Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#include <efuse.h>
+#include <boot_if.h>
+#include <sha512.h>
+#include <boot_desc.h>
+#include <errno.h>
+#include <device.h>
+#include <drivers/tpm/tpm-v2.h>
+#include "lzo/unaligned.h"
+
+#define LOG_LEVEL CONFIG_HABANALABS_LOG_LEVEL
+#include <logging/log.h>
+LOG_MODULE_REGISTER(gaudi2_tpm_auth);
+
+#define TPM_NV_DEV_SN_ADDRESS		0x01c10100
+#define TPM_NV_DEV_SN_OFFSET		0
+
+struct tpm_dev_id {
+	union {
+		u32 efuse_data[2];
+		u64 tpm_data;
+	};
+};
+
+#if DT_NODE_HAS_STATUS(DT_INST(0, tcg_tpm2_tis_spi), okay)
+#define TPM2_DEVICE DT_LABEL(DT_INST(0, tcg_tpm2_tis_spi))
+#else
+#define TPM2_DEVICE ""
+#endif
+
+__weak int tpm2_pcr_extend(const struct device *dev, uint32_t index,
+		uint32_t algorithm, const uint8_t *digest, uint32_t digest_len)
+{
+	return 0;
+}
+
+__weak int tpm2_nv_read_val(const struct device *dev, uint32_t index,
+		uint32_t offset, void *data, uint32_t count)
+{
+	return 0;
+}
+
+static const struct device *get_tpm2_device(void)
+{
+	const struct device *tpm2_dev;
+
+	tpm2_dev = device_get_binding(TPM2_DEVICE);
+	if (tpm2_dev == NULL)
+		LOG_ERR("tpm2 binding failed for %s device", TPM2_DEVICE);
+
+	return tpm2_dev;
+}
+
+static int preboot_get_efuse_dev_id(struct tpm_dev_id *efuse_dev_id_ret)
+{
+	int ret;
+
+	ret = preboot_fuse_read(EFUSE_DEV_ID_BACKUP_OFFSET_1, &efuse_dev_id_ret->efuse_data[0]);
+	if (ret)
+		return ret;
+
+	ret = preboot_fuse_read(EFUSE_DEV_ID_BACKUP_OFFSET_2, &efuse_dev_id_ret->efuse_data[1]);
+	if (ret)
+		return ret;
+
+	return 0;
+}
+
+int tpm_startup(void)
+{
+	int ret;
+	const struct device *tpm2_dev = get_tpm2_device();
+
+	if (!tpm2_dev)
+		return -EOPNOTSUPP;
+
+	ret = tpm2_startup(tpm2_dev, TPM2_SU_CLEAR);
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_ERR("TPM: tpm2_startup failed");
+		cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL);
+	}
+
+	return ret;
+}
+
+int tpm_pcr_extend(uint32_t image_index, uint8_t *hash)
+{
+	int ret;
+	const struct device *tpm2_dev = get_tpm2_device();
+
+	if (!tpm2_dev)
+		return -EOPNOTSUPP;
+
+	if (hash == NULL)
+		return -1;
+
+	ret = tpm2_pcr_extend(tpm2_dev, image_index, TPM2_ALG_SHA384, hash, SHA384_SUM_LEN);
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_ERR("preboot_pcr_extend: pcr extend failed");
+		cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL);
+	}
+
+	return ret;
+}
+
+int tpm_hash_and_pcr_extend(uint32_t image_index, void *image_ptr, uint32_t image_size)
+{
+	uint8_t hash[SHA384_SUM_LEN] = { 0 };
+
+	mbedtls_sha512_ret(image_ptr, image_size, hash, MBEDTLS_USE_SHA384);
+
+	return tpm_pcr_extend(image_index, hash);
+}
+
+void preboot_tpm_auth(void)
+{
+	int ret;
+	u64 tpm_dev_id = 0;
+	struct tpm_dev_id efuse_dev_id;
+	const struct device *tpm2_dev = get_tpm2_device();
+
+	efuse_dev_id.tpm_data = 0x0;
+
+	if (cpu_boot_err_get(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL) || !tpm2_dev)
+		return;
+
+	ret = tpm2_nv_read_val(tpm2_dev, TPM_NV_DEV_SN_ADDRESS, TPM_NV_DEV_SN_OFFSET,
+			       &tpm_dev_id, sizeof(tpm_dev_id));
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_ERR("TPM: NV_READ failed. ret = %d", ret);
+		cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL);
+	}
+
+	/* tpm device id returns as Big Endian, need to convert */
+	tpm_dev_id = sys_be64_to_cpu(tpm_dev_id);
+	LOG_INF("TPM device ID: 0x%016llx", tpm_dev_id);
+
+	ret = preboot_get_efuse_dev_id(&efuse_dev_id);
+	if (ret) {
+		LOG_ERR("TPM flow: efuse read failed. ret = %d", ret);
+		cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL);
+	} else {
+		LOG_INF("EFUSE device ID: 0x%016llx", efuse_dev_id.tpm_data);
+	}
+
+	if (efuse_dev_id.tpm_data == 0) {
+		LOG_ERR("TPM flow: error: efuse device ID not programed");
+		cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL);
+	} else if (efuse_dev_id.tpm_data != tpm_dev_id) {
+		LOG_ERR("TPM flow: error: efuse device ID is different than TPM device ID");
+		cpu_boot_err_set(CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL);
+	}
+}

b/lib/tpm/tpm_internal.h

@@ -0,0 +1,280 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0+ */
+/*
+ * Copyright (c) 2015 Google, Inc
+ */
+
+#ifndef __tpm_internal_h
+#define __tpm_internal_h
+
+static unsigned int duration_ms[TPM_DURATION_COUNT];
+
+/*
+ * Array with one entry per ordinal defining the maximum amount
+ * of time the chip could take to return the result.  The ordinal
+ * designation of short, medium or long is defined in a table in
+ * TCG Specification TPM Main Part 2 TPM Structures Section 17. The
+ * values of the SHORT, MEDIUM, and LONG durations are retrieved
+ * from the chip during initialization with a call to tpm_get_timeouts.
+ */
+static const u8 tpm_protected_ordinal_duration[TPM_MAX_PROTECTED_ORDINAL] = {
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,		/* 0 */
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,		/* 5 */
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_SHORT,		/* 10 */
+	TPM_SHORT,
+};
+
+static const u8 tpm_ordinal_duration[TPM_MAX_ORDINAL] = {
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,		/* 0 */
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,		/* 5 */
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED,
+	TPM_SHORT,		/* 10 */
+	TPM_SHORT,
+	TPM_MEDIUM,
+	TPM_LONG,
+	TPM_LONG,
+	TPM_MEDIUM,		/* 15 */
